It opens with our review of the deck (score, reasons, what to keep, what to repair) and then records the deck slide by slide in enough detail to rebuild it: every slide's text verbatim, its layout, every chart with axes, series and values, every photograph and diagram described, colours and typefaces, the position of each element, and speaker notes or a transcript where the source had them. ## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent purple and yellow identity, a clear section route from the overview on slide 3 through the five values to wellness, and a repeatable value template (slides 28, 36, 39, 43, 48) that makes the five values easy to compare. Slide 28 is readable and well organised, and the quote and statement pages (30, 35, 46, 58) land one idea each with strong emphasis. The main weaknesses are fit and legibility: body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 is very small, many section dividers (4, 6, 8, 15, 20, 22, 25, 53, 60) are nearly empty, and the 64-page length has thin or repetitive stretches. As a self-paced reference guide it is usable, but it would be hard to read if projected.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the three-column value template on slide 28 and its siblings, the single-idea statement slides with yellow highlight boxes such as 35 and 46, and the consistent palette and footer.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge the tiny body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 and cut the copy so it fits at a readable size.

**What would change the score:** Merge or remove the empty divider pages and trim the filler quote and analogy slides so the 64 pages carry more per page and the argument moves faster.

_Confidence medium. 64 of 64 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is an internal culture guide, titled SecurityScorecard DNA, shared with all employees. It opens with a mission statement framing the guide as a shared playbook, then moves through a short overview of mission, vision, the purpose of culture, the customer, three strategic goals and the organization. Its middle sections cover the five [SCORE]card values (Solutions Focused, Customer Obsessed, One Scorecard, Resilient, Embody #SecurityDNA), each with anti-patterns and behaviors by seniority, followed by innovation and experimentation, and employee health, wellness and development. It closes with a call to apply through the careers page. Slides use short headline titles, one idea per slide, and sometimes a single quotation or image-led page. Slide 13 frames competition in a few stark lines, slide 21 sets out the three goals in parallel columns, slide 28 lays out a value with its anti-patterns and behaviors in a three-column grid, and slide 57 walks through a root-cause chain as a sequence of short answers.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the SecurityScorecard DNA culture guide have?**

The SecurityScorecard DNA culture guide has 64 pages. Slide 3 gives an overview of its eight sections, from Our Mission and Vision through Employee Health and Wellness.

**What is SecurityScorecard's mission according to its culture guide?**

Slide 5 states the mission as: To make the world a safer place by transforming the way organizations understand, mitigate, and communicate cybersecurity to their Boards, employees, and vendors.

**What are the five SecurityScorecard values in the company's culture deck?**

Slide 27 lists the five values as Solutions Focused, Customer Centric OBSESSED, One Scorecard, Resilient, and Embody #SecurityDNA. Slide 26 describes them as the 5 things the company values most in its employees.

**What are SecurityScorecard's three strategic goals?**

Slide 21 sets out the three goals: drive retention and differentiation through the largest contributory network of engaged, rated organizations; enhance the value of the network through a marketplace of applications and services; and achieve ubiquity by embedding its trusted scorecards into key business workflows for Boards, Regulators, and Insurers.

## How to read the slide sections

Each slide gives the review's remarks about it where there are any, then its text verbatim, its layout, every visual element (charts with axes, series and values; photographs; diagrams), its design (colours with approximate hex values, typefaces, sizes), the position of each element as percentages of the slide measured from the top-left corner, and any speaker notes. Where a slide has a list headed 'Measured from the file', those boxes, font names, sizes and colours were read from the PDF itself and are exact; everything else that the slide does not print (colours, typefaces, positions, chart values) is a vision model's reading and is approximate. **Layout**

Left-aligned text block sits in the left half, starting about 5% from the left edge, with the title stack between roughly 22% and 45% of the slide height. A full-height photograph fills the right half, and a full-width purple band sits near the bottom-left, ending at about 56% of the width. The logo sits left-aligned inside the band with the Confidential mark below at the bottom-left. There is no slide number and no footer beyond these marks.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed studio photograph of a smiling young woman with short, wavy brown hair, wearing a white collared shirt with the sleeves rolled, arms folded across her chest, a black belt and blue jeans, standing and facing the viewer. Framing is a mid-shot cropped at the thighs and placed on the right side of the slide, with her head near the top edge. Lighting is soft, even and frontal, on a light grey seamless backdrop. Dominant colours are light grey, white, indigo and violet. Layered hexagon shapes sit behind and across her torso: a large lavender (#8B6FE0) hexagon with a dark indigo (#34217C) hexagon outline inset, and a stack of concentric indigo chevron-like hexagon bands running across her shirt and arms, partly transparent so the shirt shows through. A violet (#6641F3) horizontal band runs from the left edge to about 56% of the width at the bottom-left and is layered over the hexagon edge. The SecurityScorecard logo mark (a six-sided interlocking hexagon icon) and wordmark appear in white on the band.

Background: Light grey (about #C4C4C6) photographic studio backdrop, full-bleed. Light grey photographic background with a violet band and indigo and lavender hexagon graphics on the right. The eyebrow 'SecurityScorecard DNA' is bold indigo #34217C at about 4.9% of slide height. The subtitle 'For employees. Shared with everyone.' is regular indigo #34217C at about 4.9%. Shapes are flat with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners; the hexagons have sharp corners and solid fills at reduced opacity over the photo.

**Text**

SecurityScorecard wins because we move with brilliance
and speed on a common mission towards a shared vision.
We achieve this together by following one playbook.
This guide to  our culture is that playbook. It provides every team member with the
foundational knowledge of what it means to be a successful Scorecarder.
By following this guide, we collectively know how to make decisions,
when to take risks, and how to empower those around us to shine.
By following this guide, we win!
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Single centred column on a full-bleed dark purple slide. Headline in gold at the top, centred, spanning about 16% to 84% of the width. A full-width footer bar runs along the bottom 6.7% of the slide, holding the logo at the left, the copyright line at the right and the slide number at the far right. No title placeholder or subtitle; all text centred.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern of faint white hexagons at 3% opacity clustered across the lower-left and lower-centre of the background, partly behind the body text and the footer bar. **Layout**

Single-column composition on a full-bleed purple field. The title 'Overview' sits left of centre at mid-height (x about 7 %, y about 43 %), left-aligned. A full-width footer band runs across the bottom 6.7 % of the slide, holding the logo at bottom left, the copyright line at bottom right and the page number at the far right edge (x about 97 %).

No photographs, charts or diagrams. A decorative pattern of faint white hexagons (3 % opacity) tiles the left and lower part of the slide, partly behind the title and the bottom of the agenda list, and is partly covered by the footer band. ### Slide 4

**Text**

Our Mission
4
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
SecurityScorecard

**Layout**

Single-line title group sits left of centre at mid-height, with a line icon to its left and the title beginning at about 17% of the slide width. The layout is otherwise empty. A full-width purple footer bar runs along the bottom 6.7% of the slide, with the logo at the bottom left, copyright text at about 78% across, and the page number at the bottom right near the right margin. The hexagon pattern covers the lower-left area.

**Visuals**

Line icon of a stylised rocket or space shuttle on a launch base, drawn in outline strokes. The upper fuselage and fins are dark indigo (#34217C), and the swept wings, lower fuselage and base are violet (#6641F3). The icon sits left of the title with a small gap between them. Background pattern: a field of flat-top hexagons in the lower-left and centre-lower area, rendered as translucent white fills (opacity 0.10 and 0.03) over the grey, with darker grey outlines between them. The pattern is not cropped and is not a photograph. White SecurityScorecard logo (hexagon mark plus wordmark) in the footer bar, placed as a 2400 by 345 pixel image.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #C5C5C5 with translucent white hexagon pattern in the lower-left and centre-lower area. Flat light-grey background with a faint tonal hexagon lattice in the lower-left. The footer is a full-width violet bar. Title is violet (#6641F3), set in a bold condensed humanist sans-serif (Titillium Web Bold), sized about 7.9% of slide height, with a capital height about 5.5% of slide height. Footer copyright is white Montserrat Medium at about 1.2% of slide height. Page number is white Montserrat Regular at about 2.3% of slide height. The icon uses indigo and violet line work. No shadows, no corner radii on the title, no gradients. Hexagons have no stroke of their own; their edges are defined by the translucent fills.

### Slide 5

**Text**

Our Mission / To make the world a safer place by transforming / the way organizations understand, / mitigate, and communicate cybersecurity to their / Boards, employees, and vendors. / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 5

**Layout**

A single centred statement sits in the middle-lower part of a full-bleed dark purple slide, with the text block centred on about x 49%. A yellow arrow-tab label with the title 'Our Mission' is flush to the left edge at the top, and a full-width translucent purple footer bar runs along the bottom with the logo at the left, copyright at the right and the slide number at the far right. Left and right margins for the footer are about 2.3% and 3.1% of the width; there is no visible subtitle or other body content.

**Visuals**

Decorative honeycomb pattern: a field of flat-topped white hexagons at 3% opacity covers the lower-left and centre-bottom of the background, spanning roughly x 0 to 68% and y 44 to 100%, with some cells clipped by the slide edges and partly visible through the footer bar. Icon: a small outlined rocket or spire-like icon in dark purple #34217C with a thin stroke, placed at the right end of the yellow title tab, about x 17% y 6% w 3% h 4%. Logo: white SecurityScorecard logo (hexagonal mark plus bold wordmark), a placed image 2400 by 345 pixels, at the bottom left on the footer bar. No photographs, charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark violet-purple #34217C with a faint white honeycomb hexagon pattern at 3% opacity. Full-bleed solid dark violet-purple background #34217C with a faint white honeycomb pattern at 3% opacity. The body statement is set in Titillium Web Light, white #FFFFFF, at about 4.9% of slide height (about 0.7 of that for capitals), with the phrase 'make the world a safer place' in Titillium Web Bold, yellow #E1C048, underlined in the same yellow. The title 'Our Mission' is Titillium Web Bold, about 2.7% of slide height, in dark purple #34217C on a yellow #E1C048 arrow-shaped tab. The footer bar is violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity. The copyright line is Montserrat Medium about 1.2% of slide height, white; the page number is Montserrat Regular about 2.3% of slide height, white. Shapes have square corners apart from the pointed right end of the tab; no shadows or gradients.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#34217C]: full-slide background rectangle (Covers the whole slide. A white #FFFFFF fill of the same size lies directly beneath it and is hidden.)
- shape at x 0, y 43.7, w 67.8, h 56.3 [#FFFFFF]: decorative honeycomb pattern of flat-topped hexagons, drawn as a group of white polygons (White at 3% opacity, so the hexagons are only faintly visible against #34217C. Hexagon bounding boxes (x, y, w, h in %): (47.6, 63.2, 7.4, 24.4); (47.6, 82.8, 13.8, 17.2); (60.4, 76.3, 7.4, 11.4); (0, 76.3, 3.9, 23.7); (2.9, 82.8, 7.4, 11.4); (2.9, 69.8, 7.4, 11.4); (15.7, 56.7, 7.4, 11.4); (15.7, 43.7, 7.4, 11.4); (9.3, 76.3, 20.2, 17.9); (15.7, 63.2, 13.8, 17.9); (22.0, 50.2, 7.4, 11.4); (28.4, 69.8, 7.4, 11.4); (34.8, 63.2, 7.4, 24.4). Each set is repeated once in the file. Cells are separated by dark gaps and sit behind the body text and the footer bar.)
- shape at x 0, y 93.3, w 100, h 6.7 [#6641F3]: footer bar (Violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity, square corners, no shadow. Hexagon pattern shows faintly through it. Holds the logo, copyright and page number.)

### Slide 6

**Text**

Our Vision
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
6

**Layout**

Single-slide section divider with no grid. The title "Our Vision" sits left-aligned at about 17% from the left edge, vertically around the middle (y 46.6 to 54.5%), beside an eye icon at about 9% to 15% of width. The purple footer band runs full width at the bottom (y 93.3 to 100%), with the SecurityScorecard logo at the left margin (about 2.3%), the copyright line right of centre (x 78.4%), and the page number "6" at the right margin (x 96.8%). The upper half of the slide is empty.

**Visuals**

1. Eye icon, left of the title: a thin-line outline icon drawn in dark indigo (#34217C). It shows a horizontal almond or hexagonal eye outline with a smaller hexagon inside it in purple (#6641F3), a diamond at the centre, and four short diagonal dashes at the corners (two above, two below) suggesting a gaze or watch. Stroke is about 2% of slide height thick with square ends. Box about x 9.1, y 45.1, w 5.7, h 9.7.
2. Background hexagon pattern: a field of flat-topped white hexagon tiles at about 3% and 10% opacity covering the lower-left two-thirds of the slide, partly behind the title and icon. Tiles are arranged in a honeycomb and fade out toward the right and top. They are cut off by the footer band.
3. Footer band: solid purple rectangle (#6641F3 at 89% opacity) across the full width at the bottom.
4. Logo: white SecurityScorecard wordmark with hexagonal mark on the purple footer, full colour white, at the bottom left. Image is 2400 by 345 pixels.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #C5C5C5 with a subtle white honeycomb hexagon pattern. Light grey background (#C5C5C5) with a faint white honeycomb pattern. Title and accent colour are purple #6641F3; the eye icon uses dark indigo #34217C with purple inner hexagon. Footer is purple #6641F3 at 89% opacity with white text and logo. Title typeface is a bold, condensed humanist sans-serif (Titillium Web Bold), about 7.9% of slide height. Footer text is Montserrat Medium, about 1.2% of slide height, and the page number is Montserrat Regular, about 2.3%. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Hexagon tiles are flat white shapes with no outline.

### Slide 7

**Text**

Our Vision / To create a new language for companies and their / partners to / communicate, understand, and improve / each other’s security posture. / We want SecurityScorecard to be a part of the global / speech of every organization and government: / “What’s your Scorecard?” / 7 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Centered text block of three paragraph-style groups sits in the middle of the slide, with the first line starting near 24% of slide height and the closing quote near 63%. A yellow tab label reading “Our Vision” is pinned to the top-left edge. A full-width purple footer band at the bottom holds the SecurityScorecard logo at left, the copyright line and the page number 7 at right. The page number is in the bottom-right corner, about 3% from the right edge.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern of faint white hexagons at 3% opacity scattered across the lower-left and lower-middle of the slide, partly behind the footer band, forming a honeycomb field. A small outline eye icon with radiating tick marks sits inside the yellow Our Vision tab, drawn in #34217C. A white-and-purple SecurityScorecard logo mark with wordmark sits in the footer. No charts, photographs or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid indigo #34217C with faint white hexagon pattern at 3% opacity. Solid deep indigo background #34217C with a faint white hexagon pattern. Title tab and accents in gold #E1C048. Body text in white #FFFFFF. Tab label in indigo #34217C. Footer band in violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Body typeface is Titillium Web Regular, a sans-serif with squared-off geometry; the emphasised phrase and closing quote are Titillium Web Bold. Footer copyright and page number are Montserrat (sans-serif), Medium and Regular. Body text is about 4.9% of slide height with cap height about 3.5%; the tab label is about 2.7%. Hexagon shapes have sharp corners, no shadows, no gradients, no outlines.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Review: The main weaknesses are fit and legibility: body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 is very small, many section dividers (4, 6, 8, 15, 20, 22, 25, 53, 60) are nearly empty, and the 64-page length has thin or repetitive stretches.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the tiny body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 and cut the copy so it fits at a readable size.

**Text**

“ / Culture is not just a set of values / you put on a wall; culture is a / set of behaviors people practice. / Aleksandr Yampolskiy / CEO & CO-FOUNDER / Why Is Culture Important? / Enables decision making and innovation at scale. / ● Boosts engagement and retention. / ● Attracts the best talent around the world. / ● Fosters meaningful coworker relationships. / Drives high performance. / ● Boosts It is the foundation of every organization. / ● It helps to acknowledge that people are the most / critical asset to a company. / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 9

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a full-height portrait photograph occupies the left 0 to about 40 percent of the slide, and the right column (starting about x 37.8) holds the italic quote at top, the attribution beneath it, then a gold rule and the heading and bulleted lists in the lower right, left-aligned at x 50.8. Quote and attribution are right-aligned to about x 88 with the quote block starting at x 43.2. A full-width footer band sits along the bottom 6.7 percent, holding the SecurityScorecard logo at bottom left, the copyright line at about x 78 and the page number 9 at far right (x 96.8). No title placeholder is used; the quote is the headline.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a man in a white open-collar shirt with arms folded, head and shoulders and torso cropped at the bottom edge, smiling slightly and looking toward the camera, placed on the left side of the slide and bleeding off the bottom edge. Composition is a medium close-up, subject centred around x 22 with the head near the top edge. Lighting is soft and even, with the photo treated as a duotone in lavender and violet tones (white highlights, deep indigo shadows) so the image is tinted over the purple background. Overlay: a faint pattern of dark violet hexagons (purple tint) overlaps the lower-left of the photo and continues into the footer band. Illustration: a dotted gold line (#E1C048, dots about 0.5 percent of slide height apart) runs from the left edge near y 47 down and diagonally across the photo through the hexagons to about x 22, y 93, ending at the lower hexagons. Rule: a solid gold horizontal line (#E1C048, about 0.4 percent of slide height thick) runs from x 50.8 to the right edge at y about 49.8. Logo: SecurityScorecard white wordmark with a stylised white S-shaped hexagon mark in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Deep violet #34217C with a lighter violet gradient toward the left edge, behind a duotone photograph and hexagon pattern. Deep violet background (#34217C) with a subtle lighter violet glow toward the left (#4A2FB5). Quote text is white (#FFFFFF) TitilliumWeb Italic, light-to-regular weight, large, about 5.9 percent of slide height per line with tight line spacing. Attribution name is TitilliumWeb Bold white (#FFFFFF) about 4.0 percent; role line is TitilliumWeb Regular white in capitals about 3.0 percent. Quote marks and heading and rule are gold (#E1C048). Heading is TitilliumWeb Bold gold about 4.0 percent. Body is Montserrat, sub-headings Montserrat Bold white and bullet text Montserrat Regular white, both about 2.7 percent of slide height, with bullets as small round glyphs in indent. Footer band is #6641F3 at 89 percent opacity. Line and shape styles: rectangular full-bleed band, no rounded corners, no shadows, no gradients other than the subtle background glow.

### Slide 10

**Text**

A Strong Culture
Also Helps Us
Accomplish our mission to make
the world a safer place
Celebrate people being the best
version of themselves
Drive economic opportunity
Grow intellectually
Culture is who we are, who we want SSC to be and how we want to influence the lives
of our employees, customers, suppliers, shareholders and other constituencies
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed 16:9 slide. The left column (about 0 to 50% of width, y 0 to 82%) is white and holds a two-line title at top left (x 5.1%, y 9.5%) above a four-item list indented to x 8.7%. The right half (x 50 to 100%, y 0 to 82%) is a full-bleed photograph. A full-width dark violet band (y 73.3 to 93.3%) carries a centred two-line statement, and a full-width violet footer strip (y 93.3 to 100%) holds the logo at bottom left (x 2.3%) and the copyright line at bottom right (ends at x 94.5%). No page number is visible. Margins are about 5% left and about 5% right.

**Visuals**

Photograph in the right half of the slide: a smiling woman with curly dark hair on the left, leaning toward a smiling man with short brown hair and light stubble on the right. Both wear white collared shirts and look down at a silver-grey laptop held across the lower centre. Soft diffuse indoor daylight with a blurred background of shelves. The photo carries a violet duotone tint (about #6641F3 at moderate opacity) that makes the image lavender-grey. It is cropped and full-bleed to the right edge and to the top of the slide. Over the top-right corner is a purple outline graphic of hexagons (stroke about #6641F3, about 0.5% of slide height), with its top-right hexagons running off the slide edge. Lower-left of the white panel is a honeycomb pattern of about 21 light grey (#F3F3F3) hexagons, partly cut off at the left edge, bottom edge and the photo boundary. Logo: white SecurityScorecard wordmark with a hexagonal icon at bottom left, on the violet footer.

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF in the upper left, with the right-hand photograph and a dark violet (#34217C) band across the lower part of the slide. ### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: The main weaknesses are fit and legibility: body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 is very small, many section dividers (4, 6, 8, 15, 20, 22, 25, 53, 60) are nearly empty, and the 64-page length has thin or repetitive stretches.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the tiny body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 and cut the copy so it fits at a readable size.

**Text**

SCORECARDERS SHARE: / What does / it mean to / work at SSC? / Being a Scorecarder means waking up every day knowing you are going to face a / new challenge; that you are going to solve a problem. It is having that mindset that / it isn’t just going into work and doing the same thing as yesterday and going home. / Business Development Lead (he/him) / There is no attitude of “that’s not my job, that’s not my role”. A lot of times you have / to wear several hats when you work here. So even if it isn’t part of your day-to-day, / you help wherever you are needed to get a problem solved. Sales Engineer (she/her) / Fast-paced, customer-centric, bottoms-up innovation, cultural resilience to processes: / values people first, intolerance for waste/red tape. Engineering Director (he/him) / SSC is thinking differently in how security is implemented. SSC fits into larger security / ecosystems in the way other security companies do not. Leadership is transparent / about how employees fit in the bigger picture, which works in an environment where / you can go execute your ideas. Product Marketing Manager (she/her) / Since it is a fast-pace and fast-growing company, there's lots of career growth / opportunities. Executive Assistant (she/her) / Culture is the fabric of the company: the living, breathing, day-to-day moments / that every person in the company can contribute to. Culture makes the company / what it is. Executive (she/her) / 11 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. A full-height violet panel occupies the left 30.2% of the slide and holds the label and title, left-aligned at about 5% from the left edge. The right area, starting at about 35% from the left, holds a single left-aligned quotation block of body paragraphs with a large opening quotation mark above it. A thin footer band runs across the bottom 6.7% with the SecurityScorecard logo at bottom left, the copyright line and slide number 11 at bottom right. No title sits at the top; the label sits at mid-height on the left panel.

**Visuals**

A faint pattern of white hexagon outlines at about 3% opacity, arranged in a honeycomb cluster across the lower part of the dark right area and along the bottom edge. A large opening quotation mark glyph in violet #6641F3 sits above the body text. A white rectangular label box behind the SCORECARDERS SHARE: text. The SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagonal icon with wordmark) at bottom left, 2400 by 345 pixels, boxed in the footer band. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Deep indigo #34217C, with a violet #6641F3 panel covering the left 30.2% and a violet footer band. Two-tone violet and deep indigo palette with gold accents. Background deep indigo #34217C. Left panel violet #6641F3. Footer band violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Title text white #FFFFFF with gold accent words in #E1C048. Body text white #FFFFFF with bold gold #E1C048 role titles. Label text indigo #34217C on white #FFFFFF box. Typefaces: title and label in Titillium Web Bold (sans-serif, condensed, bold); body in Montserrat Regular (geometric sans-serif) with Montserrat Bold for role titles; copyright in Montserrat Medium. Title height about 6.9% of slide height per line; body text about 2.5% of slide height; label about 2.5%; footer text about 1.2% to 2.3%. Hexagon shapes have sharp edges, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 12

**Text**

We’re backed by the best investors in Tech.
They have high expectations for us to be sustainable, profitable and
efficient.

We share those expectations and hold ourselves accountable!

That’s why we have to run fast!

12
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Single centred column of three text groups stacked vertically in the middle band of the slide, with a short violet divider rule between the second and third groups. No title, subtitle or bullets; the text is centred on the slide's horizontal axis. A full-width violet footer band runs along the bottom 6.7% of the slide, with the SecurityScorecard logo at the left margin (about 2.3%), the copyright line right-aligned near x 78-94%, and the page number '12' at the far right (about 96.6%).

**Visuals**

Non-photographic decoration only. A tiled field of semi-transparent white hexagonal cells (about 3% opacity, so they read as a faint lighter purple) fills the lower-left and lower-centre of the slide, from roughly y 43% to the footer, with the largest cluster between x 0% and 60%. Some cells overlap and merge into irregular honeycomb clusters, and a few cells sit partly behind the footer band. One thin horizontal violet rule (#6641F3) sits centred at about y 57% between x 43% and 57%. One raster image: the SecurityScorecard logo (2400 by 345 px), white hexagonal icon plus white 'SecurityScorecard' wordmark, placed in the footer at the left. No charts, tables or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark purple #34217C with faint white hexagon pattern overlay. Flat, dark purple background (#34217C) with no gradient. Main statement text is white (#FFFFFF) Titillium Web Regular, sans-serif, with key phrases in bold gold (#E1C048). The closing line 'That’s why we have to run fast!' is Titillium Web Bold in white, with 'fast!' in bold gold. Text size is about 4.9% of slide height for all statement lines (cap height about 3.5%). Copyright and page number are Montserrat in white at about 1.2% and 2.3% of slide height. Footer band is violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Divider rule is a solid violet line about 0.5% of slide height thick, square ends, no shadow. Hexagons have no outline and no shadow.

### Slide 13

**Text**

We are excited to create and expand a new market / But our competitors are just as hungry / We must work smarter, harder and more efficiently than the rest / We dare to win / “ / There is no second place, there’s only first place. / Roger Bannister / SecurityScorecard / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Two-column split: the left column (about 0 to 50% width) holds a left-aligned text block with a quotation mark and grey hexagon pattern below it, and the right half (50 to 100% width) is a full-height, full-bleed photograph. A full-width dark band occupies the lower 27% of the slide, carrying a large one-line quotation with the attribution on the same baseline, and a thin full-width purple footer bar at the bottom holds the logo at left and the copyright line at right. No title, no slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph (right half, x50 y0 w50 h100, full-bleed to top, right and bottom edges, unboxed): a black-and-white press photograph of a male runner in a light vest with a chest emblem reading AAA and a race bib numbered 41, mid-stride with arms spread and mouth open as he breaks a finish tape stretched diagonally across the frame. Behind him spectators in suits and jackets look on, a stone castle tower and a flag stand in the background, and a race official with a clipboard is at lower right. Lighting is flat daylight. The photo carries a full purple duotone tint (roughly #6A55C8 highlights to #2A1F5C shadows). Overlaid at top right is a purple outline of hexagons (x84.8 y0 w15.2 h30.9), stroke #6641F3 about 2 px wide, partly over the photo and partly off the right edge. Grey hexagon pattern (about 22 flat-filled hexagons, fill #F3F3F3, no outline, forming a honeycomb cluster in the lower left of the white panel, from x0 to x68 and y44 to 100, partly hidden behind the dark band). Quotation mark glyph in #6641F3 at x4.1 y68 w5.5 h17.8, large. Logo: SecurityScorecard white hexagonal icon with white wordmark at x2.3 y94.8 w14.2 h3.6.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF panel on the left and a tinted photograph on the right, over a dark indigo #34217C band and purple #6641F3 footer. White background panel on the left; dark indigo band #34217C across the lower part; purple footer bar #6641F3 at about 89% opacity (appears roughly #7B52F5). Body text is black #000000 Montserrat Medium, about 3.46% of slide height per line (cap height about 2.4%). Accent words are Montserrat Bold in #6641F3. The quotation is in Titillium Web Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 5.93% of height, with 'first place.' in Titillium Web Bold Italic in gold #E1C048. The attribution is Titillium Web Regular, white, about 3.95%. Copyright is Montserrat Medium, white, about 1.23%. Line and shape style: flat fills, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners; a thin bright purple rule (#6641F3) runs along the top edge of the dark band. Body text has generous line spacing and paragraph gaps.

### Slide 14

**Text**

How do we / implement / culture at SSC?
SSC Onboarding & Boot Camp
Culture Assets
Monday All Hands (don’t forget to shoutout!!)
Town Halls & [SCORE] Values Champions
How we work everyday!
How do we / measure success / with culture at SSC?
Employee Engagement Surveys (eNPS anchored)
Kudos Slacks
Retention Rate
Your Feedback!
SecurityScorecard
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
14

**Layout**

Two-panel split: a narrow purple left column (about 32% of width) holds two stacked title blocks, each with a short gold rule above it, left-aligned at about 5% from the left edge. The wider dark-purple right panel holds two groups of list items: a top group with a chevron marker beside it, and a lower group with a second chevron marker. The footer band runs full width along the bottom 6.7% of the slide, with the logo at lower left, the copyright line right of centre, and the page number at lower right. There is no slide title in the header; the titles sit mid-height in the left column.

**Visuals**

Two solid purple chevron arrows (right-pointing, chevron shape, fill #6641F3, no outline, no shadow) sit on the dark right panel. The upper chevron is at about x 35.9%, y 19.4%, w 5.3%, h 14.2%, aligned with the first list group. The lower chevron is at about x 35.9%, y 65.3%, w 5.3%, h 14.2%, and overlaps a cluster of faint hexagons. About 13 flat hexagon shapes in white at 3% opacity form a decorative honeycomb pattern across the lower-left of the right panel and the bottom of the left panel, partly behind the lower chevron and the footer band. The hexagons do not carry text. The SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagonal mark with white wordmark) sits in the footer at lower left, placed as an image 2400 by 345 pixels. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background is a two-tone purple: the left column is #6641F3 (vivid violet) and the right panel is #34217C (deep indigo) with a white underlay beneath it. Titles are white #FFFFFF and gold #E1C048 for emphasis words, set in a bold condensed humanist sans (Titillium Web Bold) at about 4.9% of slide height in capitals-and-lowercase with normal letter spacing. Body list text is white #FFFFFF in a geometric sans (Montserrat Regular) at about 3.0% of slide height, with generous line spacing (about 7% of slide height between items). Accents are gold #E1C048 rules (about 0.6% of slide height thick) above each title, and purple #6641F3 chevrons. The footer band is #6641F3 at 89% opacity. The copyright line is Montserrat Medium at about 1.2% and the page number Montserrat Regular at about 2.3%. Shapes are flat with square corners, no gradients and no shadows.

### Slide 15

**Text**

Our Customers
SecurityScorecard
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
15

**Layout**

Single full-bleed slide with no grid. The title 'Our Customers' sits left-aligned at about 7% from the left edge, with its baseline in the lower-middle area (about 47% to 55% of slide height). A full-width footer band runs along the bottom 6.7% of the slide, with the SecurityScorecard logo at the left margin and the copyright line followed by the page number at the right.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern only: a field of semi-transparent white hexagons (about 3% opacity) across the lower-left and lower-middle of the slide, partly behind the title and the footer band. The hexagons are flat-topped with pointed left and right sides and are arranged in a honeycomb cluster; a few hexagons are stacked vertically in the centre-right. Logo: the SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagonal mark with the white wordmark 'SecurityScorecard') at bottom left, placed as a 2400 by 345 pixel image. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid violet #34217C with faint white hexagon pattern. Solid deep violet background #34217C with a faint tonal hexagon pattern. Title in white #FFFFFF, bold humanist sans-serif (Titillium Web Bold), large, about 7.1% of slide height. Footer band in violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity, with white #FFFFFF text. Footer copyright in Montserrat Medium at about 1.2% of slide height; page number in Montserrat Regular at about 2.3% of slide height. Hexagon shapes are white at 3% opacity with no outlines, no shadows, no gradients and no rounded corners. Accent colour is the violet #6641F3 footer band.

16

**Layout**

Full-width white content area below a dark indigo hexagon-patterned header band that occupies the top quarter of the slide, separated by a thin violet rule. The two-line title is centred in the header. Seventeen logos sit in four loosely staggered rows of about four to five across the white area, centred on the slide with generous margins. A full-width violet footer bar runs along the bottom: the SecurityScorecard logo at left, the copyright line and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Seventeen company logos, no photographs or charts. Left to right, top to bottom: Row 1: Toyota (red wordmark #EB0A1E under a silver emblem), Pfizer (white script wordmark on a blue #0093D0 oval), jetBlue (navy #003876 wordmark), Nokia (blue #124191 wordmark), Bumble (yellow #FFC629 hexagon with a bee-like icon, wordmark in yellow). Row 2: T-Mobile (magenta #E20074 serif wordmark with a T-mark), vimeo (cyan #1AB7EA lowercase wordmark). Row 3: American Cancer Society (white ribbon-and-sword emblem on a red #C8102E band beside a blue #0034A0 block with white text), Microsoft (four-square logo in red, green, blue and yellow with grey #737373 wordmark), SAP (white wordmark on a blue #008FD3 trapezoid with registered mark), lululemon (red #D31F26 circle with white symbol), Pepsi (red, white and blue #004B93 globe with blue wordmark). Row 4: stockX (dark grey #333333 wordmark with green #00C167 X arrow on white), Bob’s Discount Furniture (red and yellow cartoon mascot above red-outlined wordmark), vodafone (red #E60000 ring with red wordmark), SKECHERS (navy #0B2F6B S-mark with grey shading above navy wordmark), A+E Networks (black letters A, plus sign, E with 'NETWORKS' below). Behind the logos is a pattern of light grey hexagons (#F3F3F3). Logos are flat, unshadowed and not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a light grey #F3F3F3 hexagon pattern in the lower content area and a deep indigo #2B1A6E hexagon-patterned band across the top. White #FFFFFF content area. Header band in deep indigo purple #2B1A6E with a subtle darker hexagon texture. Violet accent #6641F3 used for the header rule and footer bar. Title line 1 in white #FFFFFF and line 2 in gold #E1C048, both TitilliumWeb-Bold sans-serif, bold weight, about 6.9% of slide height. Footer text in Montserrat sans-serif, white, about 1.2% to 2.3% of slide height. Shapes are flat with no corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 17

**Review notes**

- Review: The main weaknesses are fit and legibility: body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 is very small, many section dividers (4, 6, 8, 15, 20, 22, 25, 53, 60) are nearly empty, and the 64-page length has thin or repetitive stretches.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the tiny body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 and cut the copy so it fits at a readable size.

**Text**

Customer Reviews
“
The Most Compelling Solution of Cyberscoring in the Market
SecurityScorecard is a strong platform, which supplies a compelling service. Only SecurityScorecard has both scoring and questionnaire management, which makes this solution a unique one in the market. The team behind it is absolutely professional and service oriented.
CEO, Services
“
SecurityScorecard Has Played a Key Role in How We Evaluate Peers, Vendors, and Customers
SecurityScorecard has been an integral part of our quarterly risk assessment process. It allows us to evaluate ourselves against our industry peers.
It has also helped us risk rank potential vendors and customers.
Information Security Analyst, Services
“
Helps Manage Risk and Reduces the Significant Overhead of 3rd Party Reviews
We are almost a year into the tools use and have seen significant value related to managing risks as well as reducing the overhead of performing 3rd party reviews, versus our old process of email and MS Office document questionnaires.
Director Of Cybersecurity & Infrastructure, Energy
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
17

**Layout**

Three equal text columns sit under a single left-aligned title near the top, with the columns starting at about 6.6%, 39% and 71.4% of the slide width and a left margin of about 6.6%. Each column stacks a large quotation mark, a bold heading, a short purple rule, body text and a bold attribution. A full-width purple footer bar runs along the bottom with the logo at the left, the copyright line and the slide number 17 at the right.

**Visuals**

None photographic. Decorative pattern: a field of flat hexagonal tiles in white at 3% opacity spread across the dark purple background, partly cropped at the left and bottom edges and passing behind the body text of the columns. Three large opening quotation marks (“) in violet #6641F3, one above each column. Three short horizontal violet rules (#6641F3) separating headings from body text. Footer bar with the SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagonal icon and wordmark, 2400 by 345 pixel image).

**Design**

Background is deep purple #34217C with a faint white hexagon pattern at 3% opacity. Title and column headings are white #FFFFFF in a bold condensed sans-serif (Titillium Web Bold), title at about 6.9% of slide height and headings at about 3.5%. Body text is white #FFFFFF in a regular geometric sans-serif (Montserrat Regular) at about 2.2% of slide height, with key phrases in bold Montserrat #E1C048 gold. Attributions are Titillium Web Bold white at about 2.6%. Quotation marks are Montserrat ExtraBold in #6641F3. Rules are solid #6641F3, about 0.5% of slide height thick. Footer bar is #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Copyright line is Montserrat Medium at about 1.2%, and page number is Montserrat Regular at about 2.3%. Shapes have square corners and no shadows. A hidden run reading 'and Utilities' is set in #34217C, the same as the background, so it is invisible.

### Slide 18

**Text**

It’s important that every Scorecarder understand our mission, vision and the / customers we serve and reflect this in the work they do every day. / We believe everyone can perform at their best and make the largest impact / if they know how their team and individual goals / fit into the big picture. / 18 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Full-bleed single-slide statement with no title; the two paragraphs of centred text sit in the vertical middle of the slide, with the upper paragraph's first line starting near 29% of slide height and the lower paragraph ending near 61%. A footer band runs across the full width at the bottom (y 93.3% to 100%) holding the logo at the left margin, the copyright line at the right and the page number at the far right. Upper area above the text is empty.

**Visuals**

A decorative pattern of semi-transparent white hexagons (about 3% opacity) scattered across the lower-left and lower-centre of the background, partly behind the footer band. Hexagons are flat-sided with dark gaps between them, some combined into clusters. The SecurityScorecard logo is placed in the footer at the bottom left: a white hexagonal mark followed by the white wordmark 'SecurityScorecard' in bold sans-serif. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background solid deep purple #34217C with a faint white hexagon pattern at about 3% opacity. Body text is white #FFFFFF in TitilliumWeb-Regular, about 4.9% of slide height, centred. Emphasised phrases are TitilliumWeb-Bold in gold #E1C048, underlined with a gold rule under the text baseline. Footer band is a violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity across the full width, height about 6.7% of slide height. Copyright text is Montserrat-Medium about 1.2% of slide height in white; page number is Montserrat-Regular about 2.3% of slide height in white. Line spacing is generous, about 1.5 times the font size. Sans-serif, no shadows, no rounded corners, no gradients.

### Slide 19

**Text**

What’s the job of a / goalkeeper? / Not to STOP the ball. / To WIN the game. / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 19

**Layout**

Two-panel split layout. A solid purple left panel (0 to about 46% of the width) holds a centred two-line question at mid-height, with a purple chevron bridging into a full-height photograph on the right (about 23% to 100% of the width) that carries a centred two-line statement. There is no title placeholder at the top; the text sits near the vertical middle. A full-width purple footer bar runs along the bottom 6.7% with the logo at left and the copyright and slide number at right. Margins are about 4% on the left for the question and about 2% from the bottom for the footer text.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a goalkeeper lying face down on grass inside a goal net, arms extended forward, wearing a dark blue jersey, white gloves and a dark helmet-style cap, with a bright stadium background out of focus. The photo is cropped to fill the right panel from about x 23% to 100% and y 0% to 93.5%, with a purple tint and semi-transparent hexagon net overlay (#34217C tint, roughly 40 to 50% opacity). Dominant colours are muted violet, grey-white and dark green grass. Lighting is soft and diffuse. A purple chevron (#6641F3, solid, no outline) points right across the seam between the two panels. A decorative outline hexagon cluster in purple (#6641F3 strokes) sits in the top-right corner, overlapping the photo edge and cropped by the slide edge. The footer logo is the white SecurityScorecard mark (hexagonal icon plus wordmark).

**Design**

Background is solid deep indigo #34217C on the left, with a faint pattern of white hexagons at 3% opacity. Right side is a tinted photograph. Titles and body are white (#FFFFFF) in Titillium Web Bold, a humanist sans-serif, with a cap height of about 4% of slide height and text lines about 6% of slide height. Accent words STOP and WIN are yellow gold #E1C048 in the same bold face. Accent colour is violet #6641F3 for the chevron, the footer bar (at 89% opacity) and the decorative hexagons. Slide number and copyright are in Montserrat, a geometric sans-serif, regular and medium weight, white, at about 2.3% and 1.2% of slide height. Shapes are sharp-cornered with no shadows; the chevron and footer have flat fills with no gradients.

### Slide 20

**Text**

Our 3 Strategic Goals / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 20

**Layout**

A single left-aligned title sits in the lower-middle of the slide, starting at about 7% from the left edge, with the upper half left empty. A full-width violet footer band runs along the bottom 6.7% holding the SecurityScorecard logo at bottom-left, the copyright line at bottom-right and the page number '20' at the far right. A faint honeycomb pattern fills the lower-left and lower-middle area behind the title and above the footer.

**Visuals**

Decorative only, no photograph, chart or diagram. A tiled honeycomb pattern of 19 white hexagonal shapes at 3% opacity sits behind the title, mostly in the lower-left to lower-middle area and continuing under the footer band, cropped at the slide edges and at the footer. Logo: the SecurityScorecard logo in white, a hexagonal mark with the wordmark 'SecurityScorecard' beside it, placed in the footer at bottom-left. Footer band: a violet rectangle at about 89% opacity.

**Design**

Background: Flat solid violet #34217C covering the full slide. Full-bleed flat deep violet background #34217C with no gradient. ### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The three strategic goals sit in parallel numbered columns, each with a bold lead phrase and one supporting sentence, which makes the set easy to scan.
- Review: The main weaknesses are fit and legibility: body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 is very small, many section dividers (4, 6, 8, 15, 20, 22, 25, 53, 60) are nearly empty, and the 64-page length has thin or repetitive stretches.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the tiny body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 and cut the copy so it fits at a readable size.

**Text**

These Goals Guide / Our Actions and / What We Do / 01 / Drive retention and differentiation / through the largest contributory / network of engaged, rated / organizations, who provide / inside-out information for their / scorecards. / 02 / Enhance the value of the network / through a marketplace of / applications and services / on top. / 03 / Achieve ubiquity / by embedding our trusted / scorecards into key business / workflows for Boards, / Regulators, and Insurers. / 21 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark purple slide with the title block set in a left column, vertically centred at about 38 to 58 percent of the height. Three numbered goal groups sit in a diagonal stagger to the right: group 01 top-centre, group 02 middle-right, group 03 lower-centre, each with a dotted polygon outline, a large number, a short rule, a bold label and a descriptive text block to its right. A full-width footer bar runs along the bottom 6.7 percent, with the SecurityScorecard logo at bottom-left (x about 2.3 percent) and the copyright line and page number 21 at bottom-right (page number right edge about 97.8 percent). Margins are roughly 2 to 4 percent of the width.

**Visuals**

Three dotted-outline polygons in violet #6641F3, each an irregular hexagon-like outline with a dotted stroke, drawn as image graphics: the top one at x 34.2 y 1.2 w 18.8 h 33.1, the middle one at x 45.5 y 32.2 w 18.8 h 33.1, and the lower one at x 32.0 y 58.6 w 18.8 h 33.1 (each source image 2048 by 2035 pixels). Behind the slide sits a honeycomb pattern of large white hexagons at about 3 percent opacity, concentrated in the lower-left and lower-centre, partly cropped by the left and bottom edges. Three short solid violet rules under the numbers, roughly 7.6 percent wide and 0.4 percent tall, at about x 39.8 y 15.3, x 51.0 y 45.6 and x 37.5 y 72.1. A solid footer bar in violet #6641F3 at 89 percent opacity across the full width at the bottom. A white SecurityScorecard logo (icon mark plus wordmark) at bottom-left, image 2400 by 345 pixels at x 2.3 y 94.8 w 14.2 h 3.6. No photographs; the composition is flat and graphic with no lighting or shadows.

**Design**

Background is a flat deep indigo-purple. Title lines are set in white #FFFFFF except the words Actions and and What We Do which are gold #E1C048. Large numbers 01, 02 and 03 are violet #6641F3. Group labels and descriptive text are white #FFFFFF; the bold emphasis words are set in bold weight within white text. Title typeface is Titillium Web Bold, a sans-serif with squared-off terminals, at about 5.9 percent of slide height with cap height about 4.2 percent. Body text is Montserrat Regular with Montserrat Bold for emphasis, about 2.5 percent of slide height. Labels are Montserrat Bold about 2.5 percent. Page number is Montserrat Regular about 2.3 percent; copyright is Montserrat Medium about 1.2 percent. Polygon outlines are dotted violet strokes with no fill; the rules are solid violet bars; no shadows, gradients or rounded corners on text, and footer bar is a plain rectangle.

### Slide 22

**Text**

Who makes up our organization? / SecurityScorecard / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 22

**Layout**

Single-column composition with no grid. The title sits left-aligned at about 7% from the left edge, in the lower-middle of the slide, roughly between 47% and 55% of the height. A full-width violet footer band occupies the bottom 6.7%, with the logo at the left margin (about 2.3%), the copyright line right-aligned near 94%, and the slide number at the far right (about 96.5% to 98%).

**Visuals**

Decorative background of 20 faint white hexagon shapes at 3% opacity, arranged as a honeycomb pattern. Most cluster in the lower-left and lower-middle of the slide, with two tall hexagon pairs near x 60% to 68%. They sit behind the title and extend under the footer band, which overlaps them at 89% opacity. The SecurityScorecard logo at bottom left is a white hexagonal emblem followed by the white wordmark SecurityScorecard. No photographs, charts, or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid indigo-violet #34217C with a faint white honeycomb hexagon pattern at 3% opacity. Solid deep indigo-violet background with a faint tonal honeycomb pattern. Title is white (#FFFFFF) Titillium Web Bold, a humanist sans-serif, at about 7.1% of slide height, with tight, normal spacing. Footer text is white Montserrat, sans-serif: Medium for the copyright at about 1.2% of height and Regular for the slide number at about 2.3%. The footer band is violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity, with no shadow, gradient, or rounded corners. Hexagon shapes are white at 3% opacity with no outlines.

### Slide 23

**Text**

Our Founders / Dr. Aleksandr Yampolskiy / After a decade working at companies like Gilt Groupe, / Oracle, and Goldman Sachs, Aleksandr Yampolskiy quickly / grew SecurityScorecard to its current standing, being the / global leader in cybersecurity ratings. / Sam Kassoumeh / After 5 years at Gilt Groupe, Sam Kassoumeh co-founded / SecurityScorecard with the goal to provide organizations / with a true 360-degree view of risks, currently making it / the only service with over 5 million companies / continuously rated. / “ / We spent a week brainstorming on the name for a company. None of the domains were / available. So we randomly picked Jigsar and incorporated in Delaware. We changed the / name to SecurityScorecard in a few months. We move and pivot fast. / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 23

**Layout**

Two equal columns sit below a full-width photo band, with the founder portraits at upper left and upper right and the title Our Founders right-aligned at the right of the band. Each column has a bold name heading above a body paragraph, both left-aligned with a left margin of about 5% and a right column starting at about 52.6%. A full-width dark purple quote panel spans the lower part of the slide, with a purple rule above it and a purple footer bar at the bottom holding the logo at left and the copyright line and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Two monochrome purple-tinted portrait photographs of men in head-and-shoulders framing, cropped to rectangles. Left: a man with short dark hair, smiling slightly, wearing a light open-collar shirt, facing the camera; the image is duotone lavender. Right: a bearded man with curly dark hair and rectangular glasses, smiling, wearing a light open-collar shirt, facing the camera; duotone lavender. Both photographs are full-height of the photo band and tinted lavender over a pale band. Top right corner has a decorative outline of purple hexagons (about 3 to 4 overlapping outlined hexagons, stroke purple) bleeding off the right edge. Behind the lower white area is a pattern of pale grey #F3F3F3 flat hexagons, filled, partly overlapping and cut off by the quote panel. A large purple quotation mark glyph sits at the top-left corner of the quote panel, overlapping the purple rule. Logo: SecurityScorecard white logo mark and wordmark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a lavender #EDEBF8 band across the top 36% and a dark purple #34217C panel across the lower part of the slide. White background with a pale lavender #EDEBF8 photo band at top and a dark purple #34217C quote panel below. Title Our Founders is bold geometric sans (Montserrat Bold) in dark purple #34217C, about 4.9% of slide height for the cap-height line. Name headings are condensed bold sans (Titillium Web Bold) in purple #673EF3, about 4.0% of height. Body text is Montserrat Medium in black #000000, about 2.5% of height, with generous line spacing. Quote text is Titillium Web Bold in gold #E1C048, about 3.5% of height, with the final sentence in Titillium Web Bold Italic in white #FFFFFF. Accent colour is violet #6641F3, used for the rule, quotation mark, and footer bar at 89% opacity. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients; hexagon outlines are thick purple strokes.

### Slide 24

**Text**

Our Team: We Promote a Work From Anywhere Culture
SSC is a global organization | We have Scorecarders in 21 countries across 5 continents
Where are we located?
• Our HQ is in NY, but your office is anywhere.
• We operate as a remote-first organization, even if we are in the office!
Our Great Remote Meeting Culture
Work-From-Anywhere Culture
• Have an agenda in the invite
• We support Scorecarders to be successful because we trust them to take control of their work and schedule - wherever that may be
• Have a Zoom ID in the invite (Remote first!)
• $500 WFA stipend for every employee
• Send out actions after the meeting is over
• We operate on Eastern Standard Time, but are accommodating to all when possible.
• Respect everyone’s time
• Cameras are preferred but optional with no explanation needed for internal meetings
24
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a purple header band across the top 18% holding a centred title and a centred subtitle line. Below, the middle band has a 'Where are we located?' heading at left, a globe illustration at centre and two bullet points at right. The lower area has two columns: 'Our Great Remote Meeting Culture' at left starting near x 8%, and 'Work-From-Anywhere Culture' at right starting near x 54%, both with bulleted lists top-aligned at y 58%. A purple footer band at the bottom holds the SecurityScorecard logo at left, the copyright line and the page number 24 at right. Margins are about 8% on the left for body columns and about 2% on the right for the page number.

**Visuals**

Illustration: a line-drawn globe in dark grey (#3F3F3F-like) outline with simplified continents, centred at about x 40-55%, y 22-52%, circular, with no fill. Five purple map pins (#6641F3) sit on the globe, four on the upper-left and right portions and one over the Atlantic-facing upper right; they overlap the globe outline. Decorative hexagon pattern: a cluster of purple outline hexagons (#6641F3, stroke about 2px) in the top right corner, cropped by the slide edge, overlapping the header band edge. Light grey (#F3F3F3) filled hexagons form a honeycomb pattern behind the lower half of the slide, cropped by the footer band and the left and right edges, sitting behind the text. Logo: white SecurityScorecard hexagon mark with wordmark at bottom left in the footer band.

**Design**

Background white (#FFFFFF) with a purple (#6641F3) header and footer band. Title in TitilliumWeb Bold, a condensed humanist sans-serif, white #FFFFFF, about 4.9% of slide height, with the subtitle in TitilliumWeb Bold yellow #E1C048 for the first phrase and Montserrat Regular white #FFFFFF for the rest at about 3.0%. Section headings in TitilliumWeb Bold purple #673EF3 at about 3.5%. Body text in Montserrat Regular black #000000 at about 2.5% with bold runs in Montserrat Bold. Page number and copyright in Montserrat in white. Lines and shapes: hexagon outlines in purple, stroke about 2 px, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners.

### Slide 25

**Text**

Our [SCORE]card Values
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
25

**Layout**

Single-title section layout with no grid. The title sits left-aligned at about 7% from the left edge, in the lower-middle band of the slide, with the upper half left empty. A full-width purple footer bar runs along the bottom with the logo at the left, the copyright line at the right and the page number at the far right.

**Visuals**

Decorative tessellated honeycomb pattern of flat-topped white hexagons at 3% opacity, tinted purple over the background. It fills the lower-left and lower-middle area, sits behind the title and continues under the footer bar, and is cropped at the left, bottom and right edges. The only other visual is the white SecurityScorecard logo in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid violet #34217C with a faint white hexagon honeycomb pattern at 3% opacity. Solid deep violet background with a faint white hexagon honeycomb. Title in white TitilliumWeb-Bold, a condensed humanist sans-serif, at about 7.1% of slide height. Footer text in white Montserrat, a geometric sans-serif: copyright in Medium at about 1.2% and page number in Regular at about 2.3%. Footer bar is violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity. No gradients, shadows or corner radii; hexagons are flat-filled.

### Slide 26

**Text**

WHAT ARE
[SCORE]card
VALUES
[SCORE]card values are the 5 things we value most in our employees and expect every Scorecarder to live by daily.
26
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Two-column split: a narrow left column (about 0 to 37% of width) holds the title WHAT ARE, right-aligned near the column edge, above a tiled pattern; the wider right column (about 37 to 100%) holds the large [SCORE]card title right of centre and the VALUES subtitle right-aligned beneath it, with the body sentence left-aligned at about 41% of width in the lower-middle area. A full-width violet footer band runs along the bottom with the SecurityScorecard logo at bottom left, the copyright line and page number 26 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

A full-bleed geometric lattice pattern in the left column: interlocking hexagon and star outlines drawn in violet #6641F3 with thick strokes on a dark indigo ground, covering roughly 0 to 37% of width and from about 24% to 93% of height. The right column carries a faint, low-contrast version of the same hexagonal lattice in slightly lighter violet over the dark indigo panel. A thin gold accent bar (about #D4B53A) sits above the WHAT ARE title, spanning about x 13.2 to 43.6 and y 9.5 to 10.1. The SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagonal mark with wordmark) sits in the footer at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Dark indigo #34217C with a faint violet hexagonal lattice pattern across the whole slide, with a violet #6641F3 block behind the top-left title area.

### Slide 27

**Text**

[SCORE]card / VALUES / Solutions Focused: We identify problems then quickly / shift to solutions. / Customer Centric OBSESSED: We are OBSESSED with / making our customers happy. / One Scorecard: We are one team that embraces diversity, / fun and collaboration. / Resilient: We preserve through obstacles. / Embody #SecurityDNA: We practice what we preach. / SecurityScorecard / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 27

**Layout**

Single slide on a full-bleed purple field with a large centred serif title at the top spanning about 22% to 79% of the width. Below it, a rotated VALUES label sits at about x 22.5% and a left-aligned column of five bold-lead statements starts at x 28% and runs to about 74%, with a vertical rhythm of roughly 7% of slide height between entries. A full-width footer band at the bottom (y 93.3% to 100%) holds the SecurityScorecard logo at the left margin (x 2.3%), the copyright line at x 78.4% and the page number 27 at x 96.5% on the right.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern of 14 distinct hexagon shapes (the file records each one twice, at identical positions) in the middle and lower-left of the slide, drawn as flat, slightly lighter purple tiles at 3% white opacity, partly overlapping the text column and the footer band, with no outlines or shadows. Full-width footer band in violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity over the hexagon pattern. SecurityScorecard logo at bottom left: a white hexagonal mark with a wordmark reading SecurityScorecard in white bold sans-serif, placed as a 2400 by 345 pixel image at x 2.3%, y 94.8%, w 14.2%, h 3.6%. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid indigo #34217C with a faint white hexagon pattern at 3% opacity. Solid deep indigo background #34217C with a faint white hexagon tessellation at 3% opacity. Title in white #FFFFFF, a high-contrast Georgia-style regular serif, roughly 16.8% of slide height for the cap height block, with brackets around SCORE. VALUES in gold #E5BC41, Montserrat Medium uppercase, rotated 90 degrees, about 6.9% of slide height. Body copy in white #FFFFFF Montserrat Medium at about 3.0% of slide height with generous line spacing (about 1.2). Lead-in labels in Montserrat Bold gold #E5BC41. Accent treatments: 'Centric' struck through in the gold label, 'OBSESSED' in bold white with an underline. Footer band #6641F3 at 89% opacity; copyright in Montserrat Medium about 1.2% of slide height; page number in Montserrat Regular about 2.3%. No gradients, shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 28

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Each value uses one grid with a definition, anti-patterns and behaviors by seniority, turning an abstract value into concrete examples.
- Review: The deck has a consistent purple and yellow identity, a clear section route from the overview on slide 3 through the five values to wellness, and a repeatable value template (slides 28, 36, 39, 43, 48) that makes the five values easy to compare.
- Review: Slide 28 is readable and well organised, and the quote and statement pages (30, 35, 46, 58) land one idea each with strong emphasis.
- Worth keeping: Keep the three-column value template on slide 28 and its siblings, the single-idea statement slides with yellow highlight boxes such as 35 and 46, and the consistent palette and footer.

**Text**

01 / Solutions / Focused / We propose solutions, not just identify problems / We act now, not later / We don’t make excuses, we get it done / We build on top of ideas, we don’t tear them down / Anti Patterns / (Things to Watch Out For) / Starts with “no because” instead of “yes and” / Analysis paralysis: doesn’t get from thinking to doing / Points out problems without moving on to solutions / Being too flexible and too nice when “dependent teams” tell you to wait. Provide realistic deadlines and ask for affirmation. / Running frazzled, and doing too many things. / Forget to ask “How can I help?” / Behaviors / in Action / Senior Leader: Knows when to pivot the team to bigger and better opportunities. Communicates Clearly. “What is your 1 thing?” / Manager: Clears the path - empowers & enables their team members to bring up any issue and drive a solution. / Individual Contributor: Uncovers an issue, proactively brings it to the attention of the team with a proposed solution and then executes to take it over the finish line. / Team: EMBRACE CHANGE / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 28

**Layout**

Two-panel slide. A full-height deep purple panel occupies the left 30.6% of the slide with the section number, title and statements left-aligned at about 5.1% from the left edge. The right area is white and holds two equal text columns, Anti Patterns at x 33.9% and Behaviors in Action at x 65.1%, each with a chevron header and a horizontal rule beneath it. A purple footer bar runs the full width at the bottom with the logo at left and copyright and slide number at right. The title sits top-left in the purple panel; column headings sit at the top of the white area.

**Visuals**

Decorative line art and shapes only; no photographs or charts. Top right: a cluster of purple outline hexagons cropped by the top and right slide edges. Behind the text in the lower-middle white area: a cluster of 22 flat light grey (#F3F3F3) hexagons forming a honeycomb pattern, partly cut off by the purple footer bar and the left purple panel. Two solid purple (#6641F3) right-pointing chevrons, one beside each column heading. Two thin horizontal rules: a gold rule at left under the number 01 and a purple rule under each column heading. Logo: white SecurityScorecard logo (hexagon mark and wordmark) at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: white #FFFFFF in the main area, deep purple #34217C left panel, violet #6641F3 footer bar at 89% opacity. Titles: gold #E1C048 for 01 and Solutions, white #FFFFFF for Focused, violet #6641F3 for column headings. Body text: black #000000 for column copy, white #FFFFFF for left panel statements. Accents: violet #6641F3 for speaker labels and Team; bold runs in black. Typefaces: Titillium Web Bold (condensed sans-serif) for titles and column headings; Montserrat Regular, Bold and Medium (geometric sans-serif) for body, footer and page number. Title text about 6.9% of slide height; column headings about 3.5%; body about 2.5%; footer about 1.2%. Line spacing is tight; paragraphs separated by one blank line. Shapes have square corners; no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 29

**Text**

At Securityscorecard, / we are all responsible for owning our dependencies. / We operate with “Get-It-Done” urgency. / We win by winning fast. / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 29

**Layout**

A single centred text block of four lines sits in the middle of the slide, spanning roughly x 19.6% to 80.4% and y 36% to 62%, with the body lines centred on the slide's vertical axis. There is no conventional title. A full-width footer band runs across the bottom 6.7% of the slide, with the logo at the left margin, the copyright line right of centre, and the page number at the right margin.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern of semi-transparent white hexagons (about 3% opacity) scattered across the lower-left and lower-centre of the background, forming a honeycomb-like cluster that partly sits behind the text block. The hexagons are flat-sided, filled white at 3% opacity, with no outline. In the footer, a white SecurityScorecard logo (a stylised hexagonal mark followed by the wordmark) at the left. No charts, tables or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid colour #34217C (deep violet) with faint white hexagon pattern at 3% opacity. Solid deep violet background #34217C with a faint white hexagon pattern. Headline and body text is white #FFFFFF, bold and regular weights of Titillium Web (a sans-serif with squared, technical letterforms). Emphasis phrase 'owning our dependencies.' is bold, underlined, in gold #E1C048. Closing line 'We win by winning fast.' is bold gold #E5BC41. Body text size is about 4.9% of slide height. Footer band is violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Footer copyright and page number are set in Montserrat (geometric sans-serif); page number regular at about 2.3% of height, copyright medium at about 1.2%. No shadows, gradients or corner radii.

### Slide 30

**Text**

Urgency
is not
just addressing the
squeakiest wheel.
SecurityScorecard
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
30

**Layout**

Centered stacked headline in four lines on a white slide, with the text block centred horizontally around x 50%. A short purple bar sits above the headline at the top centre. 'is not' sits on a purple block at the centre. The bottom 6.7% is a purple footer band holding the logo at left, copyright text centred-right and the page number at far right. Light grey hexagon pattern fills the lower left and centre; purple outline hexagons fill the top-right corner. No margins are defined beyond the centred text block.

**Visuals**

Illustration: decorative pattern of light grey (#F3F3F3) flat filled hexagons, about 23 filled hexagons tiled in a honeycomb arrangement across the lower-left and lower-centre of the slide, from roughly x 0% to 68% and y 43.7% to the footer. Hexagons overlap and are separated by white gaps, with some cropped by the left edge and the footer band. Shapes are flat with square corners, no gradients, no shadows.

### Slide 31

**Text**

A true
sense of
urgency
is one born
of both
empathy
and
confidence
.
Urgency is not emergency or panic.
Urgency is a shared attitude of what needs to
happen now and how we can make that
happen together. Urgency does not disappear
when an “urgent” task is completed. Urgency is
the lack of complacency, and to quote one of
our employees
“...If you feel comfortable, you’re probably
doing something wrong, you’re probably
moving too slow … you should
always be trying something new.”
31
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. A full-height violet panel occupies the left 30% of the width and holds a large bold title, left-aligned at about 5.9% from the left edge and starting at about 15% of the height. The right area (about 30% to 100% of the width) holds a left-aligned body text block starting at about 41.7% from the left and 23.7% from the top, with a bold quote block below it after a blank line. A dark footer band runs across the full width from 93.3% to 100% of the height, carrying the logo at the left, the copyright line at the right and the page number at the far right.

**Visuals**

Decorative only, no photographs, charts or diagrams. A pattern of thirteen flat-top hexagons, filled white at 3% opacity, sits over the dark background on the right side and under the footer band, partly hidden by the left panel. The footer band is violet at 89% opacity, so the hexagons show faintly through it. One logo: the white SecurityScorecard logo (hexagonal mark with the wordmark) at bottom left, placed as a 2400 by 345 pixel image.

**Design**

Background is dark indigo #34217C with a violet #6641F3 left panel and footer band. Title text is white #FFFFFF with the word urgency in gold #E1C048. Body text is white #FFFFFF with the emphasised phrases in gold #E1C048. The quote is bold white #FFFFFF. Accent colour is gold #E1C048. Title typeface is Titillium Web Bold, a humanist sans-serif, at about 6.9% of slide height per line with tight leading. Body and quote typeface is Montserrat, a geometric sans-serif: Regular for the main body, Bold for the emphasised phrases and the quote, Medium for the copyright, at about 3.7% of slide height for body and quote and about 1.2% for the copyright. Lines are unruled; shapes are plain rectangles and hexagons with square corners and no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 32

**Text**

Which of these shows urgency?
A.
B.
Urgency means prioritization and relentless focus.
Be productive, not busy. Be forward thinking, not reactive.
Don’t have meetings just to have meetings, focus on getting work done.
Respect each other’s time.
32
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Single slide with a white upper area holding the title top-left and two side-by-side calendar screenshots labelled A and B, and a dark indigo lower band holding four centred lines of body text. The title sits at about 5.6% from the top and 5.9% from the left. The screenshots occupy roughly the middle band from 23% to 67% of the height, with a footer strip along the bottom about 6.7% high containing the logo at left and the copyright and page number at right, both sitting at the same baseline.

**Visuals**

Two screenshots of Google Calendar week view, August 2021, showing Sun 1 to Sat 7 with GMT-04 time labels from 7 AM to 8 PM. Screenshot A (left, labelled A.) is dominated by solid blue events. Mon 2: project review 9:30am; Weekly All Hands Meeting 11am (https://securityscorecard link); extra meeting 12–1pm; extra meeting 1–3pm; Monday planning meeting 3–4pm. Tue 3: project review 9:30am; Weekly catch up 10–11am; extra meeting 12–1pm; extra meeting 1–3pm; bi-weekly team meeting 4–5pm. Wed 4: project review 9:30am; metrics 10am–12pm; extra meeting 12–1pm; extra meeting 1–3pm; extra meeting 3–5pm. Thu 5: project review 9:30am; extra meeting 10am–12pm; extra meeting 12–1pm; extra meeting 1–3pm; team outing 4–5pm. Fri 6: project review 9:30am; extra meeting 10–11am; extra meeting 11am–12pm; extra meeting 12–1pm; extra meeting 1–3pm; Friday project retrospective 3–4pm. Screenshot B (right, labelled B.) uses mixed colours: blue for work events, green for breaks, lavender for personal events, and one dark blue event. Mon 2: project review 9:30am; Weekly All Hands Meeting 11am (blue); break / eat / stretch 1–2pm (green); Monday planning meeting 3–4pm (blue). Tue 3: project review 9:30am; Weekly catch up 10–11am (struck through, white box with blue outline); break / eat / stretch 1:30–2:30pm (green); bi-weekly team meeting 4–5pm (blue); pick up kids 6–7pm (lavender). Wed 4: project review 9:30am (blue); metrics 10am–12pm (dark blue #3F51B5 approx); break / eat / stretch 12:30–1:30pm (green). Thu 5: project review 9:30am (blue); break / eat / stretch 2–3pm (green); team outing 4–5pm (blue); pick up kids 6–7pm (lavender). Fri 6: project review 9:30am (blue); break / eat / stretch 1–2pm (green); Friday project retrospective 3–4pm (blue). Both screenshots are flat UI captures with light grey window borders, soft drop shadows, white calendar grids and thin grey hour lines. Two violet decorative label tabs with gold letters sit over the top-left corners of each screenshot. A purple hexagon outline graphic sits at top right. A white SecurityScorecard logo sits bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF in the upper area, with a dark indigo #34217C band from about 60% to 93% of height and a violet #6641F3 footer strip. White upper background with a dark indigo (#34217C) lower band and a violet (#6641F3, 89% opacity) footer strip. Title is black (#000000) TitilliumWeb-Bold, about 5.2% of slide height, left aligned. Body text is white (#FFFFFF) Montserrat, Regular for three lines and Bold for the second line, about 3.0% of slide height, centred. Accent colours are gold #E5BC41 for the A and B letters and violet #6641F3 for the label tabs, footer and hexagon outlines. The calendar screenshots are boxed with thin light grey borders and soft drop shadows; the label tabs are square-cornered rectangles. Light grey #F3F3F3 hexagon fragments sit at the left edge at mid height. Sans-serif typography throughout.
