### Slide 33

**Text**

Low drag, high speed. / Germany’s Autobahn, in large part, adheres / to the popular conception of having no speed limit while / still boasting significantly fewer road fatalities than U.S. / highways. / This is not to suggest that a lack of rules / and regulations (the autobahn still has many) leads to / better outcomes, but rather that a system designed to / enable both velocity and predictable outcomes is the / most scalable. / Is an outdated rule blocking your great idea? Let’s revise it! / Is there a guideline missing that would allow faster, more / consistent decisions? / Let’s create it! / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 33

**Layout**

Two-column split layout: a solid indigo text panel fills the left half (about 0 to 50% of the width) and a full-height photograph fills the right half (about 50 to 100%). The title sits top-left at about 13.6% from the top, left-aligned at x 5.1%, with a 5% left margin. The body text block starts at about 30% from the top and is left-aligned in a single column; the closing line Let’s create it! sits below it at about 74.5%. A full-width violet footer bar runs across the bottom 6.7% of the slide, holding the logo at bottom left, the copyright line and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

1) Photograph (right half, visible area about x 49.9 to 100, y 0 to 93.3): a long-exposure night view of a curving multi-lane highway seen from an elevated angle. Streaks of red, pink and white light from vehicle taillights and headlights trace sweeping arcs across the lanes. A bright white light glows at the far right edge where the road curves. Overhead road signs sit at the upper right with a blue-grey panel reading motorway route signage; a dark tree line and a pale purple-grey dusk sky sit at the top of the frame. Composition is full-height and cropped, with the road running diagonally from lower left toward upper right. The image is tinted by a translucent violet-indigo overlay (about 40 to 50% opacity), giving the whole photo a muted lavender cast. Dominant colours: lavender grey #7A6F9A, dusk violet #5E4E8F, warm red-pink light #E06A7A, white highlights #F4F0FF. Lighting is low-key ambient dusk with bright headlight streaks. 2) Illustration (top right corner, x 84.8 to 100, y 0 to 30.9): a cluster of outlined hexagons drawn in a bright violet stroke (about #6A4BEA), some partly cropped by the top and right slide edges, overlapping the photograph. 3) Decorative pattern on the indigo panel: a field of large, low-contrast white hexagons at 3% opacity, arranged in a honeycomb of overlapping shapes behind the text, and repeated faintly in the footer bar. 4) Logo: the SecurityScorecard logo at bottom left, a white hexagonal mark with the wordmark SecurityScorecard in white bold sans-serif. No charts, tables or other diagrams.

**Design**

Background is a solid deep indigo #34217C with a faint white honeycomb hexagon pattern at about 3% opacity. The title and body text are white #FFFFFF; the accent word high and the closing line Let’s create it! are gold #E5BC41. The footer bar is violet #6641F3 at about 89% opacity. The title is set in TitilliumWeb-Bold, a humanist sans-serif, at about 6.9% of slide height with normal capitals and no letter-spacing. The body is Montserrat-Regular, a geometric sans-serif, at about 2.5% of slide height with roughly 3% line pitch and a blank line between paragraphs. Closing line is Montserrat-Bold in gold. Copyright is Montserrat-Medium at about 1.2%; page number is Montserrat-Regular at about 2.3%. Line and shape styles: hexagons are outlined in violet with no fill; no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners. The photograph is a hard-edged rectangle with no border.

### Slide 34

**Text**

If you can make a strong decision with 80% of / the data, make the decision and move forward / instead of spending time to gather and analyze / the last 20%. / Have a / Bias Towards / Action / Gather data but don’t get stuck in / analysis. Have a bias towards action / and know that it is ok to take a / calculated risk, instead of wasting / time. / It is better to take a step forward and then be / nimble and ready to adjust when you learn the / final 20% or new information that points you in / a new direction than to slow down. / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 34 / SecurityScorecard

**Layout**

Free-form staggered composition on a white slide. Three purple hexagon icons step down diagonally from upper centre to lower left, each paired with a text block to its right, except the bottom block which sits to the right of its hexagon. The bold black title 'Have a Bias Towards Action' sits left of centre at about 12.7% from the left edge, vertically centred between the first and second icons. A full-width purple footer band runs along the bottom 6.7% of the slide, carrying the SecurityScorecard logo at bottom left, the copyright line at bottom right and the page number '34' at the far right edge. Left margin about 12.7%, right margin about 8%.

**Visuals**

Three purple hexagon icons, each with a dotted purple outline hexagon offset and rotated behind it. Icon 1 (top, centred near x 35.5 to 47.7, y 6.9 to 31.8): white bar chart icon of three ascending vertical bars with the tallest on the right, sitting on a common baseline. Icon 2 (middle, x 43.9 to 56.1, y 35.3 to 60.2): white circular arrows icon, two arcs with arrowheads forming a refresh or cycle symbol. Icon 3 (bottom, x 32.2 to 44.4, y 59.1 to 84.0): white map pin icon with a purple dot in its centre. Each hexagon is flat-topped? No: pointed-top, solid fill #6641F3, unrounded corners. Dotted outline hexagons are thin dotted purple strokes at roughly 2 to 3 px width, rotated about 15 degrees relative to the solid hexagon. Behind the content is a faint honeycomb pattern of light grey #F3F3F3 hexagons with white gutters, mostly in the lower-left and lower-centre area, partly covered by the footer band. The footer band is a purple gradient strip from #6641F3 at about 89% opacity. The SecurityScorecard logo is a white hexagonal mark with white wordmark. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF solid background, with a light grey #F3F3F3 honeycomb pattern at lower left. Clean white background with a light grey honeycomb pattern at lower left. Title text black #000000 with the word 'Bias' in purple #6641F3. Body text black #000000 with bold lead-in phrases in purple #6641F3. Accents are purple #6641F3 for hexagon icons, dotted outlines and footer band. Title typeface is Titillium Web Bold, a condensed humanist sans-serif, weight bold, about 6.9% of slide height (cap height about 4.8%). Body typeface is Montserrat, a geometric sans-serif, Medium weight with Bold for lead-ins, about 2.96% of slide height. Footer copyright in Montserrat Medium about 1.23%, page number in Montserrat Regular about 2.28%. Shapes have sharp corners, no shadows, no outlines on solid hexagons. Footer band has a subtle gradient, lighter purple toward the centre.

- shape at x 0, y 93.3, w 100, h 6.7 [#6641F3]: Footer band, purple gradient bar full width (Opacity 0.89, subtle lighter-purple gradient toward the centre, no border, square corners, sits behind footer logo, copyright and page number.)

### Slide 35

**Text**

It is better to ask / for forgiveness, than / ask for permission. / 35 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / SecurityScorecard

**Layout**

Centred three-line headline sits in the middle band of a white slide, horizontally centred with a short purple accent bar above it. A honeycomb pattern of purple outline hexagons occupies the top-right corner and a light grey honeycomb pattern the lower-left; a full-width purple footer band runs along the bottom with the logo at left and the copyright and slide number at right, with small margins of about 2 percent.

**Visuals**

Decorative only, no chart, table or photograph. (1) Top-right corner: a purple outline honeycomb cluster, stroke #6641F3, about 2 to 3 px line weight, of interlocking hexagons cropped by the top and right slide edges. (2) Lower-left: a field of 22 flat light grey (#F3F3F3) hexagons with white gutters, partly cropped by the left and bottom edges, sitting behind the headline. (3) Purple accent bar #6641F3 centred above the headline. (4) Two solid yellow #E5BC41 rectangles that highlight 'forgiveness,' and 'permission.' behind the white bold italic words. (5) Bottom footer band in #6641F3 at about 89 percent opacity. (6) White SecurityScorecard logo at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with light grey #F3F3F3 hexagon pattern in the lower left and purple outline hexagons in the top right. Plain white #FFFFFF background with light grey #F3F3F3 honeycomb decoration and purple #6641F3 honeycomb outlines. Headline text is black #000000 in a geometric humanist sans-serif (TitilliumWeb Regular) at about 14 percent of slide height. Emphasised words are white #FFFFFF TitilliumWeb Bold Italic on #E5BC41 yellow blocks. Accent colours are purple #6641F3 and yellow #E5BC41. Footer text is white in Montserrat (Regular for the number, Medium for the copyright). Shapes are flat, square-cornered, with no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 36

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a consistent purple and yellow identity, a clear section route from the overview on slide 3 through the five values to wellness, and a repeatable value template (slides 28, 36, 39, 43, 48) that makes the five values easy to compare.

**Text**

02
Customer
Obsessed
We are obsessed with solving our customer’s problems and providing them value
We are always listening to our customers
We serve all our customers, internal & external, with honesty, ethics and mutual trust
We set high self-goals & standards and always keep ourselves accountable to those standards
Anti Patterns
(Things to Watch Out For)
Puts own ideas before a customer’s requests
Works on tech because it is cool vs valuable
Doesn’t communicate with stakeholders
Behaviors
in Action
Senior Leader: Meets with customers themselves, regardless of role, and able to drive the results they need.
Manager: Willing to lend their team’s efforts across the organization to meet a customer’s deadlines.
Individual Contributor: Able to speak at a high level about cybersecurity to clients and industry leaders directly to meet their needs.
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
36

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide beside a full-height dark purple left panel covering the left 30.6 percent of the width. The left panel holds the section number 02, a gold rule, the title Customer Obsessed and body copy stacked top to bottom with left alignment at x 5.1. The white area holds two columns: Anti Patterns starting at x 33.6 and Behaviors in Action starting at x 63.9, each with a purple chevron, centred heading, purple rule and text below. A purple footer band runs across the bottom 6.7 percent with the logo at left, copyright at right and page number 36 at far right; left margin about 5 percent.

**Visuals**

Background decoration: a honeycomb of flat-top light grey (#F3F3F3) hexagons, unoutlined, arranged in a cluster at the bottom centre of the white area, partly cut off by the footer band and the left panel edge. Top-right: a line-art cluster of purple (#6641F3) outline hexagons with stroke about 0.3 percent of slide height, bleeding off the top and right edges and overlapping no text. Two solid purple (#6641F3) chevron arrow shapes, one beside each column heading, pointing right. Two horizontal purple (#6641F3) rules, one under each column heading. A short gold (#E1C048) horizontal rule under the number 02 in the left panel. The SecurityScorecard logo in white at bottom left: a hexagonal icon mark followed by the wordmark SecurityScorecard in white bold sans-serif.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF in the main area with a dark purple panel #34217C on the left and a purple footer band #6641F3 at 89 percent opacity. Titles: Customer in white #FFFFFF and Obsessed in gold #E1C048, both TitilliumWeb-Bold condensed sans-serif at about 6.9 percent of slide height; column headings in purple #6641F3 TitilliumWeb-Bold about 3.5 percent. Body text: Montserrat Regular sans-serif about 2.5 percent of slide height, in white #FFFFFF on the left panel and black #000000 in the columns; bold runs used for emphasis, and the phrase solving our customer’s problems and providing them value is bold and underlined in white. Role labels Senior Leader:, Manager: and Individual Contributor: are Montserrat Bold in purple #6641F3. Accents in purple #6641F3 and gold #E1C048. Shapes are flat with square corners and no shadows or gradients. Footer copyright in Montserrat Medium about 1.2 percent, page number in Montserrat Regular about 2.3 percent.

### Slide 37

**Text**

Every company says it, but we mean it. / We are customer obsessed. / That’s why we start meetings by sharing customer stories. / Every day we put ourselves in the shoes of the millions of scored / companies who want to better understand and improve their cybersecurity / posture, not only for themselves, but for their third parties and their own / customers as well. / 37 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Single centred column on a full-bleed dark purple field. A two-line yellow headline sits in the upper third, centred, with a blank band beneath it, then a centred white body paragraph of five lines that ends above a short centred white rule. A full-width lighter purple footer bar runs along the bottom 6.7% with the white SecurityScorecard logo at the left margin, the copyright line at right and the slide number 37 at the far right margin.

**Visuals**

Decorative background pattern only: a cluster of 14 faint white hexagonal tiles at 3% opacity, arranged in a honeycomb pattern across the lower-left and lower-centre of the slide, partly behind the footer bar. Also a white horizontal rule centred below the body text and the SecurityScorecard logo in the footer (white hexagonal mark followed by the wordmark SecurityScorecard). No charts, photographs or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid #34217C deep violet with faint white hexagon pattern at 3% opacity. Solid deep violet background #34217C with faint white hexagon tiles at 3% opacity. Headline and subtitle in gold #E1C048 in Titillium Web Bold and Bold Italic; body in white #FFFFFF in Titillium Web Regular with bold underlined emphasis in Titillium Web Bold; footer bar #6641F3 at 89% opacity; footer text white in Montserrat Regular and Medium. Title cap-height-to-slide-height around 4.9% of slide height for headline, body approx 4.9%, footer about 1.2 to 2.3%. Rule is a solid white bar with square ends. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 38

**Text**

CUSTOMER / OBSESSED / The customer is always our #1 priority. / We must constantly listen to their needs. / Success for our customers is success for us. / Talk to Customers. / All work being done must be to beneﬁt customers or it shouldn't be done! / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 38

**Layout**

Two-column split: a narrow purple left column (about 0 to 36.6% of the width) with the word CUSTOMER set in its top band, and a dark purple right area holding the title OBSESSED across the top and the body statements in the lower-middle. The left column's top band ends at about 24.7% of the height, and the pattern fills the lower left. A full-width footer band sits at the bottom 6.7%, with the logo at the left margin and the copyright and page number at the right. Body text is left-aligned at about x 40.8%; the margins are about 2% to 3%.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed geometric pattern: a lattice of interlocking hexagons and six-pointed star shapes drawn as outlined violet strokes. In the left column the strokes are bright violet (#6641F3) on dark purple, fully visible and cut at the column edge at x 36.6%. Across the right area the same lattice is faint, low-contrast and tone-on-tone. The pattern is a 1920 by 1080 px raster covering the slide. A yellow horizontal rule sits above CUSTOMER at about x 10.6% to 27.9%, y 10.3%, height about 0.8%, colour about #E8B63A. The SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagonal mark with wordmark) sits in the footer at the bottom left. No photographs, charts or diagrams.

**Design**

Background is a dark violet-purple lattice pattern with a bright violet top-left block and a deep purple right panel, both slightly translucent. Titles are white (#FFFFFF): CUSTOMER in a light geometric sans (Montserrat Medium, all caps), OBSESSED in a high-contrast serif in all caps (Georgia). Body text is white Montserrat Medium with bold emphasis words (customer, always our #1 priority, listen). Accent is a yellow rule (#E8B63A). Footer band is violet #6641F3 at about 89% opacity. Title CUSTOMER is about 6.9% of slide height (cap height about 4.8%); OBSESSED about 14.1%; body text about 2.5% (cap height about 1.7%); copyright about 1.2%; page number about 2.3%. Line shapes are thin and square; no rounded corners, shadows or gradients on text. Left-column pattern fills use light grey #F3F3F3 geometric segments that sit beneath the image and are not visible.

### Slide 39

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a consistent purple and yellow identity, a clear section route from the overview on slide 3 through the five values to wellness, and a repeatable value template (slides 28, 36, 39, 43, 48) that makes the five values easy to compare.

**Text**

03 / One / Scorecard / We are one team that / embraces diversity, fun / and collaboration / We respect each other and / believe every Scorecarder brings / value / We are open to new ideas, trust / feedback from each other and / speak up when / we disagree / We say (and do) we / instead of I / Anti Patterns / (Things to Watch Out For) / Puts own needs before / the team / Doesn’t communicate / necessary information / Only wants to hear / themselves speak / Behaviors / in Action / Senior Leader: Builds a / collaborative team of diverse / thinkers and leads them to / have the best ideas. / Manager: Helps their team to / debate and commit regularly / when decisions are needed. / Individual Contributor: / Unafraid to raise solutions to / any level of the organization, / no matter / the issue. / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 39

**Layout**

Two-zone slide: a full-height dark violet panel occupies the left 30.6% of the width and holds the section number, title and four-paragraph body; the right area is white and split into two columns, Anti Patterns (x about 33.6 to 58.6) and Behaviors in Action (x about 63.9 to 88.9), each headed by a purple chevron and a horizontal rule. The section title sits top-left of the panel (03 above One Scorecard). A full-width purple footer bar runs along the bottom 6.7% with the logo at bottom-left, copyright text and slide number at bottom-right. Margins are about 5% from the left edge of the panel and the page number sits about 3.5% from the right edge.

**Visuals**

Decorative only, no charts or photographs. (1) Top-right corner: a purple outline honeycomb of hexagons, a 970 by 1030 pixel image at x 84.8 y 0 w 15.2 h 30.9, cropped at the top and right slide edges, stroked in violet #6641F3 with a thin line of about 0.3% of slide height, no fill. (2) Lower-middle: a cluster of about 22 light grey #F3F3F3 flat hexagons with white gaps, forming a honeycomb field behind and below the right column, partly covered by the footer bar, visible roughly from x 30.6 to 67.8 and y 43.7 to 100. (3) Two purple chevrons (arrow-head shapes pointing right) in #6641F3, about 4% wide by 10% high, one left of Anti Patterns and one left of Behaviors in Action. (4) Bottom-left: the SecurityScorecard logo, a 2400 by 345 pixel image in white, consisting of a white stylised hexagon-cube mark and the white wordmark SecurityScorecard.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF on the main area with a dark violet panel #34217C on the left and a violet footer bar #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Titles: gold #E1C048 for the section number and One Scorecard in Titillium Web Bold, a condensed humanist sans-serif, bold weight, size about 6.9% of slide height. Column headings Anti Patterns and Behaviors in Action in violet #6641F3, Titillium Web Bold, about 3.5% of slide height, sentence case, centred. Subtitle (Things to Watch Out For) in Montserrat Regular, about 2.0%, black #000000. Body text in Montserrat, regular and bold, about 2.7% of slide height in the columns and about 2.5% in the left panel, black #000000 in columns, white #FFFFFF in panel. Violet #6641F3 used for role labels Senior Leader:, Manager: and Individual Contributor: in Montserrat Bold. Footer text Montserrat Medium white about 1.2%; page number Montserrat Regular white about 2.3%. Rules: solid violet #6641F3 horizontal lines about 0.4% of slide height thick under each column heading; short gold #E1C048 rule under 03 in the panel. No shadows, no gradients on shapes, square corners on chevrons and rules; hexagons have no rounded corners.

### Slide 40

**Text**

With Scorecarders from / around the world we have / the competitive advantage / of bringing together diversity / of ideas and thoughts / 40 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Centered single statement set in five lines of text in the middle of a white slide, with a short purple accent bar above the statement and a full-width purple footer band at the bottom. Text is center-aligned, with a wide margin on both sides; the footer holds the logo at the left, the copyright line at the right and the slide number at the far right.

**Visuals**

Decorative outline of connected hexagons in purple #6641F3 stroke at the top-right corner, partly cropped by the right and top edges, forming a honeycomb cluster. A pale grey #F3F3F3 honeycomb pattern of filled hexagons fills the lower-left area behind the text, partly cropped by the left and bottom edges, with white gaps between cells. A short solid purple #6641F3 accent bar sits centred above the title. A solid yellow #E5BC41 rectangle highlights the phrase 'around the world' behind the text. The SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagonal mark with wordmark) sits in the purple footer at the left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background with a pale grey #F3F3F3 honeycomb pattern at lower left. White background. Headline text is black #000000 in TitilliumWeb Regular, sans-serif, with the phrase 'around the world' in TitilliumWeb Bold Italic in white #FFFFFF on a yellow #E5BC41 highlight. Body text size is about 10.9% of slide height, with lines spaced about 15% of slide height apart. Footer text is white in Montserrat, with copyright in Montserrat Medium and slide number in Montserrat Regular. Accent colours are purple #6641F3 and yellow #E5BC41. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients; the hexagon outlines are thin purple lines.

### Slide 41

**Text**

One Scorecard
The Five Dysfunctions of a Team
as described by author Patrick Lencioni
Built on Trust
Inattention
to results
Focus on
outcomes
Avoidance of
accountability
Confront
difﬁcult issues
Lack of commitment
Focus on clarity &
closure
Fear of conﬂict
Demand debate
Absence of trust
Be human
Buy this and other supporting books using your
annual educational reimbursement budget.
Read this book and understand these principles to
help us avoid dysfunctions on our teams at SSC.
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
41

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed indigo slide: the left column holds the title 'One Scorecard', a gold subtitle and white byline at top-left, with the book cover below and a centred caption at the bottom; the right column holds the title 'Built on Trust' centred over a pyramid with labels on its left, gold chevrons, and right-hand outcome labels, with a centred caption below. A full-width purple footer band holds the SecurityScorecard logo at bottom-left, the copyright line at bottom-right and the page number '41' at the far right; left margin about 5% of slide width.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a book: a red hardcover copy of 'The Five Dysfunctions of a Team' by Patrick Lencioni, shown in a three-quarter angled view with the spine on the left, slightly rotated, with a soft shadow. Cover text reads 'NEW YORK TIMES BEST-SELLER' at top, 'The FIVE DYSFUNCTIONS of a TEAM' in serif capitals, 'A LEADERSHIP FABLE' small, a black-and-white photo of a group of people in the cover centre, 'PATRICK LENCIONI' at bottom, and 'AUTHOR OF THE NATIONAL BESTSELLER THE ADVANTAGE' in tiny print; the spine reads 'PATRICK LENCIONI' vertically. Dominant colours are crimson red, white and grey, lit evenly from the front; the book is fully visible, not cropped, boxed on the indigo background with no overlay. Diagram: a pyramid made of five stacked horizontal tiers, each a purple #6641F3 band separated by thin indigo gaps, forming a triangle with the apex at top centre and a wide base at bottom. Tier labels from top to bottom: 'Inattention to results', 'Avoidance of accountability', 'Lack of commitment', 'Fear of conflict', 'Absence of trust', each centred in its tier in white bold. Five gold chevron arrows (pointing right) sit to the right of the pyramid edge, one at each tier, each pointing to a right-hand label: 'Focus on outcomes', 'Confront difficult issues', 'Focus on clarity & closure', 'Demand debate', 'Be human'. Decorative pattern: faint white hexagon tiles at about 3% opacity scattered behind the lower half of the slide. Logo: white SecurityScorecard logo (hexagonal mark plus wordmark) at bottom-left on the purple footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid deep indigo #34217C with a faint white hexagon pattern at about 3% opacity. Full-bleed deep indigo background #34217C with a faint white hexagon tile pattern at about 3% opacity; the lower footer band is purple #6641F3 at about 89% opacity. Titles are white (#FFFFFF) TitilliumWeb-Bold, a condensed bold humanist sans-serif, about 6.9% of slide height, set in capitals and lowercase as shown. Subtitle line is gold #E1C048 Montserrat SemiBold at about 2.7% of slide height; the byline is white #FFFFFF Montserrat SemiBold at the same size. Pyramid labels and outcome labels are white Montserrat Bold about 2.1% of slide height; captions are white Montserrat Regular about 2.4%. Accents: gold #E1C048 chevrons and subtitle, purple #6641F3 pyramid and footer. Shapes are flat, with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners; the book carries a soft drop shadow.

**Layout**

Centred title block sits in a full-width dark purple header band at the top, with the subtitle directly beneath the title. Below it, two stacked full-width grey bands carry the Trust Increases and Trust Decreases rows, each with its label row and arrow, followed by a four-column pillar row in the lower third on white. A purple footer bar holds the logo at left and the copyright line and slide number at right, with about 3% side margins.

**Visuals**

Two horizontal arrows form the core diagram. Upper arrow: purple #6641F3 line with a right-pointing triangular arrowhead at its right end, interrupted by the centred label Trust Increases; the words Output, Morale, Productivity, Innovation, Retention, Loyalty and Revenue sit above it, left to right. Lower arrow: dark purple #34217C line with a left-pointing triangular arrowhead at its left end, interrupted by the centred label Trust Decreases; the words Costs, Problems, Attrition, Suspicion, Time to Market and Stress sit below it, left to right. Lower section: four bold pillar labels in two-line pairs, Clarity & Compassion, Character & Competency, Commitment & Connection, Contribution & Consistency, separated by three thin vertical grey dividers. A faint light-grey hexagon lattice sits behind the lower section. The header carries a dark hexagon-outline pattern. The footer shows the SecurityScorecard logo mark with wordmark in white at left.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Header band dark indigo-purple #2B1B6E with a subtle darker hexagon-outline pattern, ending in a thin purple rule #6641F3. Title white #FFFFFF in Titillium Web Bold, about 6.9% of slide height. Subtitle gold #E1C048 in Titillium Web Bold, about 4.4%. Body text near-black #231F20 in Montserrat Regular, about 3.3%. Pillar labels #231F20 in Montserrat Bold, about 3.3%. Trust Increases label purple #6641F3 Montserrat Bold 3.5%; Trust Decreases label dark purple #34217C Montserrat Bold 3.5%. Grey bands #DDDDDD, no border, square corners, no shadow. Footer bar purple #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Copyright in Montserrat Medium 1.2%, slide number in Montserrat Regular 2.3%, both white. Arrows and lines have square ends, arrowheads are solid triangles.

### Slide 43

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a consistent purple and yellow identity, a clear section route from the overview on slide 3 through the five values to wellness, and a repeatable value template (slides 28, 36, 39, 43, 48) that makes the five values easy to compare.

**Text**

04 / Resilient / We persevere through obstacles / We don’t stop after the first try / We solve problems by experimenting and innovating / We do whatever it takes to help the team win - period / Anti Patterns / (Things to Watch Out For) / Behaviors / in Action / Stops at the first No or roadblock / Tries the same old process / again to similar results / Rejects feedback for improvement / Senior Leader: Has a plan for / when the first plan fails and / another for when that one / fails until the team succeeds. / Manager: Coaches their / direct reports to overcome / project roadblocks and deliver / the most value. / Individual Contributor: / Experiments constantly, / learns from failures, and / keeps going until the job is / completed. / SecurityScorecard / 43 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Two-zone slide: a full-height dark purple panel occupies the left 30.6% of the width above the footer bar, holding the section number, title and a four-paragraph text block; the white right area holds two columns, Anti Patterns at x 33.6% and Behaviors in Action at x 63.9%, each with a purple chevron, a purple rule beneath and body text below. A purple footer bar runs full width at the bottom 6.7%, holding the logo at left and the copyright and page number at right. Left margin of the panel text is 5.1%; the top-right hexagon outline art is cropped by the slide edges.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern only, no chart, table or photograph. (1) Light grey #F3F3F3 flat pointy-top hexagon tessellation of 22 hexagons filling the lower-middle white area from about x 30.6% to 67.8% and y 43.7% to the footer bar; partly hidden behind the left panel and the footer. (2) Purple #6641F3 outline hexagon line art (stroke about 0.4% of slide width, no fill) in the top-right corner, cropped by the top and right edges, spanning x 84.8% to 100% and y 0% to 30.9%. (3) Two solid purple #6641F3 chevron arrows pointing right, one before each column heading. (4) SecurityScorecard logo in white at bottom left: a hexagonal mark and the wordmark SecurityScorecard.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF in the right area with a deep purple #34217C panel on the left. Section number 04 and title Resilient in gold #E1C048 Titillium Web Bold, a condensed humanist sans, title size about 6.9% of slide height. Left body text is white #FFFFFF Montserrat Regular with bold runs, about 2.7% of slide height. Column headings Anti Patterns, Behaviors and in Action in purple #6641F3 Titillium Web Bold about 3.5% of slide height. Subheading (Things to Watch Out For) in black #000000 Montserrat Regular about 2.0%. Column body text black #000000 Montserrat Regular about 2.7%, with bold runs in No or roadblock and purple #6641F3 Montserrat Bold role labels Senior Leader:, Manager: and Individual Contributor:. Accent colours purple #6641F3 and gold #E1C048. Gold rule under 04 is 0.3% high. Purple rules under the column headings are about 0.3% high. Shapes are flat with no shadows, gradients or corner radii.

### Slide 44

**Text**

How do we provide feedback? / At SSC, we embrace the concept of Radical Candor to both give and / receive feedback effectively. / Feedback is the / key to continuous / improvement and / growth. / We see it is a gift / from one person / to another. / 44 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Single open canvas with no grid: a full-width dark purple header band holds a centred title and subtitle, and the white body holds two icon-and-text pairs side by side in the middle band. The slide number sits bottom right and the copyright line sits bottom right beside it, both inside a full-width purple footer bar that carries the logo at bottom left.

**Visuals**

Two purple hexagon icon badges, each with a dotted purple outline frame rotated roughly 15 degrees around it. Left badge: a white speech-bubble icon with three horizontal lines inside. Right badge: a white group-of-three-people icon. Behind both badges, a field of light grey #F3F3F3 hexagons fills the lower-left of the body. The SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagon mark plus wordmark) sits in the footer.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a dark indigo hexagon-pattern header band across the top third and a violet footer bar along the bottom. Header band dark indigo-purple with a faint darker hexagon-honeycomb pattern; a thin bright violet rule separates it from the white body. Title is white bold sans-serif (Titillium Web Bold) at about 6.9 percent of slide height. Subtitle is gold #E1C048 Titillium Web Bold at about 3.7 percent, with Radical Candor in bold italic. Body text is near-black #231F20 Montserrat at about 3.0 percent, with bold key phrases. Accents are violet #6641F3. Footer bar is violet #6641F3 at 0.89 opacity with white text. No shadows or gradients on text; icon badges are flat with no corner radius.

### Slide 45

**Review notes**

- Review: The main weaknesses are fit and legibility: body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 is very small, many section dividers (4, 6, 8, 15, 20, 22, 25, 53, 60) are nearly empty, and the 64-page length has thin or repetitive stretches.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the tiny body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 and cut the copy so it fits at a readable size.

**Text**

What is radical candor?
We base our feedback on that of author Kim Scotts’ book Radical
Candor: Be a Kick-Ass Boss Without Losing Your Humanity
• When providing feedback to your coworkers, you should
be able to challenge directly, while still caring personally
for others.
• We want you to go above and beyond the basic rules of
professionalism and really give a damn about that entire person.
Caring about that person and showing them respect is the
foundation to Radical Candor.
• When challenging directly, you must be willing to piss some people
off. It’s your job to ‘get to the point’ and
avoid using fluff. Like we said, we work fast!
• This is not a license to say any rude thing you want to anyone
whenever you feel like it. Your feedback should
be important and it should be said with genuine care
for the whole person you are delivering it to.
Ruinous Empathy
Radical Candor
Manipulative Insincerity
Obnoxius Aggression
Care Personally
Challenge Directly
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
45

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: a dark purple left panel (about 0 to 59% of slide width) holds the title at top-left, a subtitle, and four bulleted paragraphs left-aligned at x 5.1 to 6.9%; a lighter violet right panel (about 59 to 100% width, full height above the footer) holds a 2x2 matrix diagram with a vertical arrow between the columns. A full-width footer band along the bottom (y 93.3 to 100) carries the SecurityScorecard logo at bottom-left, the copyright line and the page number 45 at bottom-right, all vertically centred in the band. Left margin about 5.1%, title top at 10.5%.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a 2x2 matrix made of four rectangles with an arrow. Left column top: lavender square (#A18AF8) labelled 'Ruinous / Empathy'. Right column top: white square (#FFFFFF) with a gold outline (#E1C048, about 0.3% of slide width stroke) labelled 'Radical / Candor', the only highlighted cell. Left column bottom: lavender square (#A18AF8) labelled 'Manipulative / Insincerity'. Right column bottom: lavender square (#A18AF8) labelled 'Obnoxius / Aggression'. Each square is 12.0% wide by 21.3% tall, all with square corners and no shadow. Between the columns, a vertical arrow runs from about y 16.8% (arrowhead tip) to y 83.6% (line end) at x 79.5%; it is a dark purple (#34217C) line about 0.4% wide with a solid triangular arrowhead pointing up. The rotated label 'Care Personally' (white, bold) sits beside the arrow at x 79.0%, reading bottom to top. A small violet (#6641F3) mask rectangle at x 81.1%, y 48.7% sits on the arrow to break it behind the label 'Challenge Directly' (white, bold) placed under the Radical Candor box, centred near x 88%. No photographs or icons apart from the footer logo.

**Design**

Background is deep purple #34217C on the left and violet #6641F3 on the right panel; footer band is violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity. A faint pattern of white hexagonal shapes at 3% opacity covers the left panel and footer. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) Titillium Web Bold, large (about 6.9% of slide height, cap height about 4.8%). Subtitle is Montserrat SemiBold, white with the book title words 'Radical Candor:' in gold italic (#E1C048) and remaining title words in Montserrat SemiBoldItalic white, about 2.5% of slide height. Body bullets are Montserrat Regular, white, about 2.5% of slide height, with bold runs (Montserrat Bold) for key phrases; bullet glyphs are small gold (#E1C048) dots. Matrix labels are Montserrat Bold, black (#000000), about 2.1% of slide height. Arrow label and 'Challenge Directly' are Montserrat Bold white about 2.2%. Footer copyright is Montserrat Medium about 1.2%, page number Montserrat Regular about 2.3%. Accent colours: gold #E1C048, lavender #A18AF8, violet #6641F3, deep purple #34217C. Lines: arrow dark purple, square outline gold, no rounded corners, no gradients or shadows.

### Slide 46

**Text**

If everyone agrees, / the meeting could be better. / Encourage disagreement to see / every point of view. / But once we walk out of the room, / we disagree but commit together. / SecurityScorecard / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 46

**Layout**

Centred single-column statement stack on a white field: four lines of large text alternate between plain regular lines and bold-italic lines reversed out of yellow highlight bars, centred horizontally with the text block occupying roughly the middle 13% to 84% of the height. A short purple accent bar sits above the first line. A full-width purple footer band runs along the bottom 6.7% with the logo at left, copyright line at right and page number at the far right.

**Visuals**

Decorative and structural elements only. (1) Top-right corner: an illustration of purple outlined hexagons (line art, about 2px stroke, #6641F3) that bleeds off the top and right edges, forming a cluster of connected hexagons. (2) Lower-left background: a field of about 21 light grey (#F3F3F3) filled hexagons, flat with no outline, partly cropped by the left and bottom edges, separated by white gaps, placed behind the text. (3) Two solid yellow (#E5BC41) rectangles behind the highlighted lines, sharp corners, no shadow. (4) A short solid purple (#6641F3) horizontal bar centred above the first line. (5) The SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagonal mark plus white wordmark) at bottom left on the purple footer. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a light grey #F3F3F3 hexagon pattern in the lower left and a purple #6641F3 line-art hexagon cluster in the top right. Clean white background with purple #6641F3 accents, yellow #E5BC41 highlight bars and light grey #F3F3F3 hexagon pattern. Statement text is black #000000 Titillium Web Regular at about 8.9% of slide height (cap height about 6.2% of slide height). Highlighted lines are white #FFFFFF Titillium Web Bold Italic at the same size, reversed out of yellow boxes with about 1% horizontal padding. Footer text is white Montserrat, copyright in Montserrat Medium at about 1.2% of slide height and page number in Montserrat Regular at about 2.3%. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients. Lines are generous and centred.

### Slide 47

**Text**

We win with perseverance & innovative solutions. / Will you do what it takes to be successful? / We do not hire people who are / looking for an easy road, or just a / paycheck. / We hire people who are excited / for the hard mission ahead. / HARD ROAD / EASY ROAD / 47 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Centred two-line header block across the top band (title above, gold subtitle below), then a violet middle band holding a left-aligned two-paragraph text block on the left and two white arrow signs on the right, with a dark vertical post at about 76.5% x separating them. Footer bar at the bottom holds the SecurityScorecard logo at bottom-left, the copyright line at bottom-right and the page number 47 at the far right edge; left margin for body text is about 9.3% of slide width.

**Visuals**

Two white arrow signs on a dark vertical post. Upper sign: a white right-pointing arrow (pentagon-tip) labelled HARD ROAD in bold dark indigo Montserrat, sitting to the right of the post and running to the slide's right edge. Lower sign: a white left-pointing arrow (chevron tip) labelled EASY ROAD in bold dark indigo, sitting to the left of the post and pointing left. The post is a thin dark indigo vertical bar running from about 32% to 93% of slide height. Background photograph/pattern: a full-bleed repeating geometric pattern of interlocking hexagons and star shapes in violet and indigo tones, with lighter violet outlines, covering the top band and bottom band. SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagonal mark and wordmark) at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: deep indigo to violet tonal pattern, with a flat violet middle band (#6641F3 at about 93% opacity) and a violet footer strip (#6641F3 at about 89% opacity). Title text white #FFFFFF in TitilliumWeb Bold (condensed humanist sans-serif, bold), about 6.9% of slide height, with no letter-spacing beyond default. Subtitle gold #E1C048 in TitilliumWeb Bold, about 4.4% of slide height. Body text Montserrat Regular (geometric sans-serif) white #FFFFFF about 3.5% of slide height; the closing sentence is Montserrat Bold white. Arrow labels Montserrat Bold dark indigo #34217C about 3.5% of slide height. White arrow shapes have sharp corners, no shadow. Copyright in Montserrat Medium about 1.2% of slide height; page number in Montserrat Regular about 2.3%.

### Slide 48

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a consistent purple and yellow identity, a clear section route from the overview on slide 3 through the five values to wellness, and a repeatable value template (slides 28, 36, 39, 43, 48) that makes the five values easy to compare.

**Text**

05 / Embody / #SecurityDNA / We practice what we / preach / We are authentic to our / product’s principles / We do what we say we will / do / We hold the customer’s trust / paramount / Anti Patterns / (Things to Watch Out For) / No knowledge about / cybersecurity ratings / Answers phishing emails / with information / Leaves laptop open / and unattended / Behaviors / in Action / Senior Leader: An expert in / our industry and leads / cybersecurity talks internally. / Manager: Shows new / employees best practices for / personal cybersecurity and is / a mentor to their team. / Individual Contributor: / Able to speak intellectually / and knowledgeably about / cybersecurity and be a good / representative of SSC’s / expertise externally. / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 48

**Layout**

Two-part split: a full-height dark purple panel occupies the left 30.6% of the slide with the section number, title and left body column; the remaining white area holds two equal-width columns with chevron headers (Anti Patterns at x 33.6%, Behaviors at x 63.9%) and horizontal rules beneath them. A full-width purple footer bar runs along the bottom 6.7% with the logo at bottom-left and copyright and slide number at bottom-right; the left margin of content is about 5.1% and the top of the section number is at 8.6%.

**Visuals**

Decorative: a light grey (#F3F3F3) cluster of flat-topped hexagons forming a honeycomb pattern, placed at the lower-middle of the white area from about x 0 to 68% and y 43.7% to 100%, partly running behind the Anti Patterns column and the footer; it is not overlapped by text. A purple outline honeycomb illustration (image 970 by 1030 px, outlined hexagons in #6641F3 on white) is placed at the top-right corner from x 84.8% to 100% and y 0% to 30.9%, cropped at the right and top edges. Two purple chevron arrow shapes (#6641F3, solid, pointing right) precede the Anti Patterns and Behaviors headers. Gold and purple horizontal rules are drawn as thin lines. The SecurityScorecard logo (white wordmark with hexagonal icon, image 2400 by 345 px) sits in the footer at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background is white on the right and dark purple #34217C on the left panel. Section number and title are gold #E1C048, bold condensed sans-serif (Titillium Web Bold); the title is about 6.9% of slide height per line. Headers in purple #6641F3, Titillium Web Bold, about 3.5% of height with the subtitle in black Montserrat Regular about 2.0%. Body text is Montserrat (geometric sans-serif) regular and bold at about 2.7% of slide height, white on the purple panel and black #000000 on white, with role labels in purple #6641F3 bold. Footer bar is purple #6641F3 at 89% opacity; copyright in Montserrat Medium 1.2% white, page number Montserrat Regular 2.3% white. No shadows, no corner radii, no gradients; chevrons have square corners.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 43.7, w 68, h 56.3 [#F3F3F3]: Light grey honeycomb pattern of flat-topped hexagons, decorative, behind the content (Flat hexagons with white gaps between them, cropped by the footer bar at the bottom edge; no text overlaps.)
- image at x 84.8, y 0, w 15.2, h 30.9: Purple outline honeycomb illustration at top-right corner (Image 970 by 1030 px; purple #6641F3 hexagon outlines on white, cropped at top and right slide edges.)

### Slide 49

**Text**

EMBODY
SECURITY
#DNA
At SSC, we have a hacker’s mindset toward security.
This means we all invest the time to learn about cybersecurity,
regardless of role.
We follow our curiosity to try new paths and break things to make them
better.
We serve to make the practice of security more efficient, sustainable,
and resilient.
We operate on our customers’ trust.
Let’s work together to keep it!
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
49

**Layout**

Two-column split slide with a narrow-left label panel (about 0 to 36.6% of width, top band to 24.7% of height, then a patterned panel below to 93.3%) and a dark right panel (36.6% to 100% width) holding a large title and five-plus body paragraphs left-aligned at x 40.8%. Title SECURITY / #DNA sits top-right of the dark panel; EMBODY is right-aligned in the top-left purple block with a gold rule above it. A full-width footer band at the bottom (93.3% to 100%) carries the logo at left, the copyright line and page number 49 at right; margins about 2.3% left and 2% right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed purple geometric pattern: a repeating interlocking lattice of six-pointed star and hexagon outlines drawn as thin violet (#6641F3) lines on dark purple, visible in the lower-left panel and faintly across the whole background. Overlay panels: a solid-looking violet block (top-left, 36.6% x 24.7%, opacity 0.93) and a dark indigo panel (right, opacity 0.93) that mute the pattern behind text. A gold horizontal rule (about #E1C048, about 17.8% wide, about 0.9% tall) sits above EMBODY, with no label. Footer: a violet band (opacity 0.89) and the white SecurityScorecard logo (hexagon icon with wordmark) at bottom-left. No charts, tables or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed purple geometric lattice pattern over dark indigo #34217C with a violet #6641F3 top-left block and violet footer band.

### Slide 50

**Text**

Our managers embody our
[SCORE]card values and lead by example.
They communicate openly, define success clearly,
remove blockers and take accountability.
SSC managers are leaders who inspire others.
50
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

A single centred text block of four statements sits in the vertical middle of the slide, centred on the horizontal axis at about x 50%. There is no title placeholder. A full-width footer band runs along the bottom edge (y 93.3% to 100%) with the SecurityScorecard logo at the left, the copyright line at the right and the page number 50 at the far right. A short white horizontal rule sits below the text block.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern only: a field of tonal white hexagon cells at 3% opacity, arranged in irregular clusters across the lower-left and lower-centre of the slide and at the lower right, partly hidden behind the footer band. The file lists some hexagons twice; they are described once here. No photographs, charts or diagrams. Logo: SecurityScorecard white wordmark with hexagonal mark, bottom-left in the footer band. Rule: a solid white horizontal line, about 14% of slide width, centred beneath the text block.

**Design**

Background is a flat deep indigo purple #34217C. Titles and body text are white #FFFFFF, with emphasised phrases in gold #E1C048. The accent for the footer band is violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Typeface is Titillium Web (a squared humanist sans-serif): Regular for body lines, Bold for the gold [SCORE]card line, and BoldItalic for the gold final phrase. Text is about 4.9% of slide height (cap height about 3.5%). Footer copyright is Montserrat Medium at about 1.2% of slide height; page number is Montserrat Regular at about 2.3%. Lines are not stylised; shapes have sharp corners, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 51

**Review notes**

- Review: The main weaknesses are fit and legibility: body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 is very small, many section dividers (4, 6, 8, 15, 20, 22, 25, 53, 60) are nearly empty, and the 64-page length has thin or repetitive stretches.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the tiny body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 and cut the copy so it fits at a readable size.

**Text**

SSC Manager Principles / 01 | Solutions Focused / Bias Towards Action: Time is money. Make a decision. / Take risks. Most decisions are reversible. / Frugality: Do more with less. Empire building does not / get you ahead. / Invent & Simplify: Innovate, automate, streamline. / Undifferentiated heavy lifting is not the solution. / 02 | Customer Obsessed / Customer Obsession: Always puts customers first. / Is the voice of the customer. / Think Big: Thinking small, delivers small. Think big / to delight your customers. / Insist on the Highest Standards: Even if they / seem too high. Raise the bar and deliver high / quality products. / 03 | One Scorecard / Build and Maintain High-Performing Teams: Hire well. / Coach talent and new leaders. Move them around the / organization. / Deep Dive: Operate at all levels. Stay connected to details. / Audit metrics. Ask questions. / Earn Trust : Listen. Be candid. / Be vulnerable. Be humble. Be respectful. Be helpful. / Disagree but then Commit: Challenge decisions. If / outcome is different from original opinion, commit to it as / if your own. / 04 | Resilient / Ownership: Think like an owner. Make good / long-term decisions, not just short-term. / Deliver Results : Accountable to deliver on with the / right quality and in a timely fashion. / Be Right. A Lot : Strong judgement and good / instincts. Seek input and don’t be afraid to be / proven wrong. / 05 | Security DNA / Learn and be Curious: Constantly upleveling knowledge of / the Security and other fields. / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 51

**Layout**

Two-column text layout on a dark purple field: a title block sits at the top left inside a purple angled panel, with a left-hand column of principle headings and body text starting at about 28.6% of slide width and a right column starting at about 66.9%. Section headings run top to bottom in a staggered arrangement (01 and 02 at top, 03 and 04 in the middle, 05 at lower left), all left-aligned with consistent 2.1% body spacing. A full-width purple footer band at the bottom holds the white SecurityScorecard logo at left and the copyright line and page number at right, with margins of about 2 to 3% of width.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern of 21 white hexagon shapes at 3% opacity spread across the lower-left and centre-lower background, partly behind the footer band and text, with no overlay on the text. Logo: white SecurityScorecard logo at bottom left (image 2400 by 345 pixels), consisting of a white hexagonal interlocking mark followed by the white bold wordmark SecurityScorecard. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid deep purple #34217C with a violet #6641F3 angled panel at top left and a hexagon pattern at 3% white opacity. Solid deep purple background #34217C with a brighter violet angled panel #6641F3 at top left (rectangle clipped by a diagonal edge running from about x 34.8% at the top to about x 14% at y 37%, with the lower edge sloping down to the left). Footer band #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Title text white #FFFFFF in TitilliumWeb Light, about 5.7% of slide height, sentence case. Section headings in gold #E1C048 in TitilliumWeb Bold, about 2.7% of slide height. Bold lead-in labels and body text in white #FFFFFF in Montserrat (Bold for labels, Regular for body) at about 2.1% of slide height, with generous line gaps and paragraph gaps of about 2.5%. Copyright in Montserrat Medium, about 1.2%. Flat design, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners, no visible borders.

### Slide 52

**Text**

“
A manager’s output = the output of his or her organization + the output
of the neighboring organizations under his influence. Andy Grove
High Leverage Activities
Low Leverage Activities
• Discussing trade-offs and opportunities
with other product leaders
• Finding efficient and sustainable solutions
to old, outdated processes
• Pairing with another developer to do knowledge
transfer
• CSM taking care of customer needs leading to
greater sales for Renewals team
• Reaching out to customers to build the latest
feature
• Working on something just because the tech is cool
but with low business value
• Coding in isolation
• Delaying to meet a candidate for an interview for
non-urgent tasks
• Waiting for someone to get back to you
SecuritySccorecard
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
52

**Layout**

Two-part slide: a full-width quote band across the top 41% of the slide, and below it two text columns on the dark purple field. The left column (High Leverage Activities) starts at about x 10% and the right column (Low Leverage Activities) at about x 53%, both with headings at y 42.3% and bullets beginning at y 52.7%. A thin purple rule sits at about y 8% across the full width, with a large quotation mark at top left overlapping it. A full-width footer bar occupies the bottom 6.7%, with the logo at left (x 2.3%) and the copyright line and page number 52 at right (x 78.4% and x 96.5%). Left margin about 10%, right margin about 4%.

**Visuals**

Background: a dark purple hexagon-cell pattern photograph/illustration covering the top 41.3% of the slide (1920 by 1080 pixels, full-bleed), with dark purple-black cells and slightly lighter purple gaps, lit evenly, no people. Below it, a field of faint white hexagon shapes at 3% opacity on #34217C, concentrated in the lower-left and lower-middle areas and partly behind the bullet text. A purple horizontal rule across the full width at about y 8.1 to 8.7%. A large purple quotation mark glyph at top left. A purple footer bar with the SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagon icon and wordmark, 2400 by 345 pixels) at left. No charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background is deep purple #34217C with a hexagon-pattern photo band across the top. Quote text is white #FFFFFF in Titillium Web Regular; the phrase 'A manager’s output =' is Titillium Web Bold Italic in gold #E1C048. Column headings are white #FFFFFF in Titillium Web Bold, about 3.95% of slide height. Bullets are white #FFFFFF in Montserrat Regular, about 2.47% of slide height (sans-serif, normal weight). Accent colour is violet #6641F3 for the quote mark, the rule and the footer bar. Footer copyright is Montserrat Medium at about 1.23%; page number Montserrat Regular at about 2.28%. Flat design with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners; bullets are small round dots.

### Slide 53

**Text**

Innovation & Experimentation / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 53

**Layout**

Section divider with a single left-aligned title placed at roughly the vertical middle of the slide, starting at about 7% from the left edge. The upper half of the slide is empty background. A full-width purple footer band runs along the bottom about 6.7% of the height; the SecurityScorecard logo sits at the left of the band, the copyright line sits right of centre, and the slide number sits at the far right, all vertically centred in the band.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern of translucent white hexagons (about 3% opacity) tiled in a loose honeycomb arrangement across the lower-left and lower-centre of the slide, partly cropped by the footer band and the slide edges, with some hexagons overlapping and some hexagons appearing as paired or stacked shapes. Pattern occupies roughly the lower half of the slide and fades into the background. Logo: SecurityScorecard wordmark with a white hexagonal-cluster icon, placed in the footer band at left, white. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background solid deep indigo/violet #34217C. Title is white (#FFFFFF) in TitilliumWeb-Bold, a humanist sans-serif, approximately 7.1% of slide height, set in sentence-like title case with no caps styling. Footer band is violet #6641F3 at about 89% opacity. Copyright text is white Montserrat Medium at about 1.2% of slide height; slide number is white Montserrat Regular at about 2.3% of slide height. Hexagon shapes are white at 3% opacity with no outline, no shadows and no gradients. No accent colours beyond the footer band.

### Slide 54

**Text**

SSC’s Way
of Thinking
Most organizations overvalue great ideas.
Good ideas and bad ideas look similar
in the beginning, and we don’t know which one is
which. So we try out new ideas constantly!
Identifying root causes is a great way to look at
the deepest problems and set up great
experiments
Cheap, quick experiments > Great ideas.
54
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Single-column text layout on a white slide: a two-line title sits left of centre at mid-height, a large purple chevron separates it from a stacked block of four body paragraphs on the right, and a full-width purple footer band runs along the bottom with the logo at left and copyright and slide number at right. Left margin of the title is about 11.5% and the body block starts at about 46.4% of the width; the body is left-aligned.

**Visuals**

Decorative background pattern of 21 light grey #F3F3F3 hexagon tiles in a honeycomb cluster across the lower-left and lower-middle of the slide, with gaps between tiles showing white, partly behind the title and body text and cut off by the footer band. One solid purple #6641F3 right-pointing chevron (arrow shape) sits between the title and the body text, with its point facing right toward the body. One SecurityScorecard logo in white (hexagon mark plus wordmark) sits bottom-left in the footer band. No photographs, charts, or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a light grey #F3F3F3 hexagon pattern in the lower part of the slide. Clean white background with a light grey hexagon pattern in the lower part. Title in black #000000 Titillium Web Bold, sans-serif, with the word Thinking in purple #6641F3; title text is about 6.9% of slide height. Body text in #231F20 Montserrat Regular, sans-serif, about 3.0% of slide height, with key phrases in Montserrat Bold purple #6641F3. Accent colour purple #6641F3. Footer band is purple #6641F3 at about 89% opacity with the copyright in white Montserrat Medium and page number in white Montserrat Regular. No shadows, gradients on shapes, or rounded corners.

### Slide 55

**Text**

Think backward to think forward.
Start from your goal and map your way back to
where you are to discover the plan to get there.
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
55

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the whole slide. A two-line headline is centred across the top at about 13% from the top, and a two-line statement is centred in the lower-middle at about 68% to 80% of the height. A full-width footer bar occupies the bottom 6.7%, with the SecurityScorecard logo at the left margin (about 2.3%) and the copyright line and page number at the right, the page number at about 96.5% from the left.

**Visuals**

One photograph of a chess set, full-bleed behind all text, with a dark purple tint over it. Subject: a chess set on a board photographed at a low angle with shallow depth of field. In the foreground at the centre-left a knight (facing right, with hatched mane lines) stands beside a rook, and a pawn sits at the centre-right. Further back, bishops, rooks and pawns recede diagonally toward the upper right, blurred and fading into darkness. Composition: the board runs diagonally from lower-left to upper-right, with the foreground pieces roughly in the lower half and empty space in the upper-middle where the headline sits. Lighting: low, diffuse and even, with soft highlights on the pieces. Dominant colours: indigo #2A2357 to violet #3B2F7A, with lighter lilac #5A4FA0 on the board squares and piece highlights. The photo is not cropped and is not boxed; it is full-bleed. The purple tint is a duotone-like overlay that darkens the image toward the left edge (about #221F3F) and lightens it slightly toward the right (about #3A2A7A). Logo: white SecurityScorecard logo (hexagonal emblem plus wordmark) in the footer at the lower left. Measured but not visible on the rendered slide: full-slide white fills, several #F3F3F3 rectangles at various positions (all covered by the photograph), and a second copy of the footer fill.

**Design**

Background is a dark purple photographic treatment running from about #221F3F at the left to about #3A2A7A at the right. Headline and statement text are white #FFFFFF in a light sans-serif (TitilliumWeb Regular), with the emphasised phrases in bold italic (TitilliumWeb Bold Italic) in gold #E1C048. Body/headline size is about 4.9% of slide height, and the copyright line is about 1.2% in Montserrat Medium, white. The page number is Montserrat Regular about 2.3% in white. Footer bar is solid violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity across the full width. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on text; the footer bar has no border.

### Slide 56

**Text**

Asking why, the (at least) 5 whys, / to unearth the root cause of a problem
Often the root cause of a problem stems from a series of events, / a domino effect, with a root cause that wouldn’t be apparent by focusing / only on the final piece to fall.
Instead, as Sakichi Toyoda famously suggested / and implemented, we can candidly ask “why” in series to discover / the root cause of an issue, and more importantly, a solution.
56
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Single centred column of text on a full-bleed indigo field. The gold heading block sits in the upper third (top at about 17% of the height), followed by two white body paragraphs and a short white rule centred near 79% of the height. A translucent purple footer band runs across the bottom 6.7% with the logo at the left margin, the copyright line and slide number at the right margin. No slide title placeholder is separate from the heading block.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern of 14 low-opacity white hexagon tiles (3% opacity) scattered across the lower-left and lower-middle of the slide behind the text; the same tile set is recorded twice in the file and renders once. Logo: SecurityScorecard full-colour-reversed wordmark in white with a white hexagonal icon, at bottom left, on the footer band. One white horizontal rule (divider) centred below the body text. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background deep indigo #34217C with a faint white hexagon pattern at 3% opacity. Heading text gold #E1C048 in Titillium Web Regular with Bold Italic emphasis on 'why,' and 'root cause'. Body text white #FFFFFF in Titillium Web Regular, with 'why' in Titillium Web Bold. Text size about 4.9% of slide height for all Titillium lines (cap height about 3.4%). Footer band violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity; footer text white in Montserrat Regular/Medium at about 1.2 to 2.3% of height. Line-spacing about 5.9% of height between body lines. Flat design, no shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#34217C]: full-bleed background rectangle, solid indigo (no border; covers entire slide; a white #FFFFFF rectangle of the same size sits underneath it)

### Slide 57

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The root-cause chain builds through one question at a time, ending in a single solution line, a clear way to stage an argument.

**Text**

So why was the Washington
Monument falling to pieces?
Well, because we
were using caustic
cleaners to clean it.
WHY?
Because frankly, it was covered in pigeon poop, and
we can’t have that.
Because… there were a lot of pigeons there.
Because pigeons eat spiders, and there was an oddly
large population of spiders there.
Because as much as pigeons love eating spiders,
spiders love eating gnats, and the monument was
covered in gnats.
Because gnats are drawn to sources of light, and the
monument illuminated itself at the same time every
evening, regardless of season, attracting the gnats to
a place they’d usually otherwise ignore.
Solution –
Forget the chemicals, change the lights.
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
57

**Layout**

Two-column composition on a white slide with a full-width dark indigo footer band at the bottom. The left column holds the title, a sub-line and a 'WHY?' label flanked by two chevrons; the right column holds five bold-led paragraphs starting at about 54% of the slide width. Left margin is about 6% and the right text block ends near 91%. Text is left-aligned except 'WHY?' which is centred. The title sits top-left at about 24% from the top. A purple hexagon line-art cluster is pinned to the top-right corner. A thin purple footer strip carries the logo at bottom-left, the copyright line and the slide number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Two solid purple chevron arrows (#6641F3), one at about x 26.4% y 40.6% w 4.1% h 9.8% and one at about x 47.1% y 40.6% w 4.1% h 9.8%, both pointing right, flat-sided, no outline, no shadow, forming a left-to-right flow from the left text through WHY? to the right text. A field of light grey (#F3F3F3) flat-sided hexagons fills the lower-left background behind the body text, at about x 0% to 67.8% and y 43.7% to 100%, with white gutters between hexagons and cropped by the slide edges and the indigo band. Top-right corner: an outline illustration of interlocking hexagons drawn as purple (#6641F3) strokes of about 0.3% of slide height, unfilled, cropped at the top and right edges. Bottom-left: the white SecurityScorecard logo (hexagon mark plus wordmark) on the purple strip.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Title and body text black #000000. Accent purple #6641F3 for chevrons, WHY? and the top-right hexagon outlines. Gold #E1C048 for the Solution line. Dark indigo band #34217C with a purple top rule #6641F3. Bottom strip #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Typefaces: Titillium Web (a condensed humanist sans-serif) in Bold for the title, WHY? and the emphasised Solution phrase, and Regular for the sub-line and 'Solution –'. Montserrat (geometric sans-serif) in Regular for paragraph text with Bold lead-in words, Medium for copyright, Regular for slide number. Title about 4.7% of slide height, body paragraphs about 2.5%, left sub-line about 4.0%, Solution line about 5.9%, WHY? about 7.4%. Line spacing around 1.2 for the paragraphs, with a blank line between paragraphs. Shapes have square corners, no gradients, no shadows.

**Layout**

Single centred block of statement text on a white slide, with four text lines centred horizontally between roughly 19% and 80% of the slide height, and a gap between the third and fourth lines. A thin purple bar sits above the first line and a purple hexagon outline graphic occupies the top-right corner. A full-width purple footer band runs along the bottom 6.7% with the logo at bottom-left and the copyright line and page number at bottom-right; there is no visible title and no slide-number margin beyond the footer.

Decorative graphic only, no chart, table or photograph. Top-right corner: a cluster of purple (#6641F3) outlined hexagons, line-art with white interior, cropped by the top and right slide edges. Lower-left background: a pale grey (#F3F3F3) honeycomb pattern of flat-filled hexagons separated by white gaps, partly cropped by the left and bottom edges and running behind the text. Three yellow (#E5BC41) rectangular highlight blocks sit behind the phrases 'do not', 'employee-led!' and 'live production'. A short solid purple (#6641F3) bar sits centred above the first line. ### Slide 59

**Text**

Experiment
Regularly
5 X 5 X 5 Design by Michael Schrage
• A minimum of 5 people (or 5 teams).
• Each are given no more than 5 days to come up
with a portfolio of 5 “business experiments.”
• Should take no longer than 5 weeks to run.
• Cost no more than $5,000 to conduct.
Based on Michael Schrage’s book, The Innovator's Hypothesis:
How Cheap Experiments Are Worth More than Good Ideas
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
59

**Layout**

Two-zone split slide: a full-height violet panel occupies the left 30.2% of the width and the dark indigo area fills the rest, with a full-width violet footer band across the bottom 6.7%. The left title 'Experiment Regularly' is vertically centred in the left panel with a left margin of about 5.1%. The right column starts at about 33.3% of the width and holds the heading, a four-item bullet list, and a citation line placed lower down; the logo sits bottom-left and the copyright and page number sit bottom-right inside the footer band.

**Visuals**

Decorative pattern only, no chart, table or photograph. Across the dark right area and the footer band there is a faint tessellation of white hexagons at about 3% opacity, roughly 17 hexagon shapes of about 7 to 20% of slide width, partly overlapping and cropped at the slide edges, concentrated in the lower half of the right panel and along the footer. One logo: the white SecurityScorecard logo (a stacked hexagon emblem with the wordmark 'SecurityScorecard') at bottom-left, placed as a 2400 by 345 pixel image.

**Design**

Background is deep indigo #34217C on the right with a violet #6641F3 left panel and footer band (footer at 89% opacity). Title 'Experiment Regularly' is white #FFFFFF in TitilliumWeb Bold, about 6.9% of slide height, left aligned, two lines with tight leading. The heading '5 X 5 X 5 Design' is gold #EBC575 in TitilliumWeb Bold and 'by Michael Schrage' is white #FFFFFF in TitilliumWeb Regular, both about 6.9% of slide height on one line. Bullets are white #FFFFFF Montserrat Regular about 3.5% of slide height with round bullet glyphs and generous spacing between items. The citation's first phrase is Montserrat Bold white, the rest Montserrat Regular white, about 2.8% of slide height. Footer copyright is Montserrat Medium white about 1.2%, page number Montserrat Regular white about 2.3%. Shapes are flat with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

### Slide 60

**Text**

Employee Health & Wellness / 60 / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

Section divider with the title set left-aligned at roughly the vertical middle-low of the slide, leaving the upper half empty. A full-width footer band runs along the bottom with the logo at left, copyright text and page number at right; the hexagon pattern sits behind the lower-left area.

**Visuals**

Decorative background pattern of semi-transparent white hexagons (about 3% opacity) arranged in a honeycomb cluster across the lower-left and lower-centre of the slide, partly cut off at the bottom edge by the footer band. Also a SecurityScorecard logo (white hexagon-cluster mark with wordmark) in the footer at left.

**Design**

Background: Solid violet #34217C with translucent white hexagon pattern. Solid deep violet background #34217C with faint white hexagon outlines/fills at about 3% opacity. Title text white #FFFFFF in bold Titillium Web-style sans-serif, about 7.1% of slide height, sentence case with no letter-spacing changes. ### Slide 61

**Review notes**

- Review: The main weaknesses are fit and legibility: body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 is very small, many section dividers (4, 6, 8, 15, 20, 22, 25, 53, 60) are nearly empty, and the 64-page length has thin or repetitive stretches.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the tiny body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 and cut the copy so it fits at a readable size.

**Text**

Employee Wellness / We strive everyday to create a workplace and team that embraces diversity, fun, and collaboration. / At SecurityScorecard, there is a rich sense of community, not only as a community of colleagues, / but as a team dedicated to serving others. / We are one team, with the same objective: To make the world a safer place. We recognize that / this goal can only come about when our team works together as one unit. / Our employees are our biggest asset, and we encourage them to bring their / authentic selves to work every day. / Community Service / Employee / Recognition in HighSCORE / Employee / Interest Groups / Unlimited / Paid Time Off (PTO) / ©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved. / 61

**Layout**

Centred purple title block sits across the top edge, straddling the white header strip and the dark purple band below it. Four centred body paragraphs sit inside the dark purple band, and a row of four icons with centred captions sits in the white lower area above a full-width purple footer bar holding the logo at left, the copyright line and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Four black solid icons in one row in the lower white area, left to right: (1) a black heart-shaped icon with a handshake cut into it, above the caption Community Service; (2) a solid black five-point star, above the caption Employee Recognition in HighSCORE; (3) a solid black group of three person silhouettes (one centre person larger and in front, two outer persons), above the caption Employee Interest Groups; (4) a solid black battery icon with a lightning bolt on its right side, above the caption Unlimited Paid Time Off (PTO). Behind the icons, a pattern of light grey #F3F3F3 flat-topped hexagons with white gutters fills the lower white area, partly cropped at the slide edges. Within the dark purple band, a tiled pattern of darker hexagon outlines (about 720 by 204 px, tiled twice) covers the band. No photographs, charts or tables. The white logo at bottom left is a corporate logo.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a dark indigo purple band #2A1A6E (hexagon pattern) across the middle, light grey #F3F3F3 hexagon pattern in the lower white area and purple #6641F3 footer bar. Two-tone slide: white upper header strip and white lower content area with a dark purple band across the middle and a bright purple footer bar. Background of the band is dark indigo purple #2A1A6E with a darker hexagon outline pattern #1E1150. Title block fill #6641F3 with white bold title text. Body text white #FFFFFF in Titillium Web Regular, sans-serif, with the phrase We are one team, with the same objective: in Titillium Web Bold. Title is Titillium Web Bold, about 6.9% of slide height, body text Titillium Web Regular about 3.5% of slide height, captions Montserrat Regular about 2.0% of slide height in near-black #231F20, footer copyright Montserrat Medium about 1.2%, page number Montserrat Regular about 2.3%. Accents are #6641F3 purple and #F3F3F3 light grey pattern. Icons are solid black. Line and shape styles: flat, no shadows, no rounded corners on the title block, a thin purple rule along the lower edge of the band. Footer bar has slight transparency (opacity 0.89).

### Slide 62

**Review notes**

- Review: The main weaknesses are fit and legibility: body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 is very small, many section dividers (4, 6, 8, 15, 20, 22, 25, 53, 60) are nearly empty, and the 64-page length has thin or repetitive stretches.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the tiny body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 and cut the copy so it fits at a readable size.

**Text**

Employee Wellness
We trust you with your own time.
Including days off… take as many as you want.
We have unlimited PTO as SSC!
We know you need to take the time to rest and recharge.
You come back inspired and ready for the next challenge.
So don’t be afraid to take time off.
We even make you take time off with our Mandatory PTO Days we hold
throughout the year!
62
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.

**Layout**

A single centred title sits in a solid purple band across the top edge, with a large empty gap beneath it. Seven bulleted statements form one left-aligned column that starts at about 16.6% of the slide width and runs to about 81% of the width, placed in the upper-middle of the slide. A full-width purple footer bar at the bottom holds the SecurityScorecard logo at the left margin, the copyright line and the slide number at the right margin.

**Visuals**

A decorative honeycomb pattern of 13 semi-transparent white hexagon shapes (each 3% opacity) sits over the lower-left and lower-centre of the background, behind the footer bar and partly behind the bullet text. The hexagons are drawn as flat-top and pointy-top cells in clusters, with the largest clusters around x 0 to 62% and y 43 to 100%. The file lists the same 13 shapes twice at identical positions. There is one logo: the white SecurityScorecard wordmark with its hexagonal mark, placed at the bottom-left inside the footer bar. No photographs, charts or other illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid deep purple #34217C with a faint white honeycomb hexagon pattern.
