### Slide 63

**Review notes**

- Review: The main weaknesses are fit and legibility: body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 is very small, many section dividers (4, 6, 8, 15, 20, 22, 25, 53, 60) are nearly empty, and the 64-page length has thin or repetitive stretches.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the tiny body text on slides 9, 11, 17, 21, 45, 51 and 61 to 63 and cut the copy so it fits at a readable size.

**Text**

Employee Development
At SecurityScorecard you can grow your skills and your career.
Employee development led our Founders to where they are today, and we want to support your growth in the same way.
We provide employees with an annual educational reimbursement budget because we care about your development!
Online Learning and Webinars
Leadership Training
Tuition Reimbursement
1X1 Performance Feedback
Annual Education Reimbursement Budget Per Person
SecurityScorecard
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
63

**Layout**

Title sits in a centred purple tab that straddles the top edge of a dark purple band spanning the upper two-thirds of the slide, with the white top margin above it. Three centred body paragraphs sit in the band, and below it five equal columns of icon-over-label run across the white lower area. A full-width purple footer bar at the bottom holds the logo at left, the copyright line at right and the page number at far right.

**Visuals**

Five solid black icons in one row, left to right: (1) a browser or window icon, a rectangle with a top bar containing two dots and a horizontal three-line text block below, representing Online Learning and Webinars; (2) a crossed wrench and screwdriver, representing Leadership Training; (3) a money bag with a dollar sign, representing Tuition Reimbursement; (4) a solid five-point star, representing 1X1 Performance Feedback; (5) a graduation mortarboard cap, representing Annual Education Reimbursement Budget Per Person. Each icon is about 5 percent of slide width and sits centred above its label. Behind the lower area is a pale grey #F3F3F3 hexagon pattern on white, with hexagons of varying size partly hidden behind the icons and labels. In the upper band a dark purple hexagon honeycomb texture with darker outlines fills the background, full-bleed, with no photograph subject. Bottom-left logo: white SecurityScorecard logo mark (a hexagonal emblem) and wordmark on the purple footer.

**Design**

Background: Two-part: upper band dark purple honeycomb pattern (#241259 to #2D1A7A) over the top 67.9 percent, white #FFFFFF below, with a violet #6641F3 footer strip at the bottom. Upper band is a dark purple honeycomb pattern shading from about #241259 at left to #2D1A7A at right, with hexagon outlines in a darker #1A0B45. Lower area is white #FFFFFF with light grey #F3F3F3 hexagon outlines. Title tab is solid violet #6641F3 with white bold text. Body text is white #FFFFFF in a light-weight humanist sans (Titillium Web Regular), about 3.5 percent of slide height, with paragraph spacing. Labels are dark #231F20 Montserrat Regular about 2 percent of slide height. Title is Titillium Web Bold about 6.9 percent of slide height. Accent colour is violet #6641F3. Footer bar is violet #6641F3 at 89 percent opacity with white Montserrat text. Shapes are sharp-cornered rectangles with no shadows.

### Slide 64

**Text**

Begin your career with SecurityScorecard at
https://securityscorecard.com/company/careers
SecurityScorecard
©2022 SecurityScorecard. All Rights Reserved.
64

**Layout**

Single centred message block sitting in the lower-middle of the slide, with the headline on one line and the URL on the line beneath it, both centred horizontally. A full-width purple footer band runs along the bottom 6.7% of the slide and holds the logo at the left, the copyright line at the right, and the slide number at the far right. Margins are about 2% at the left and right edges of the footer content. The upper half of the slide is empty.

**Visuals**

A faint pattern of white hexagonal tiles at about 3% opacity covers the lower-left and lower-centre of the slide behind the text, arranged in staggered honeycomb clusters with their edges visible as slightly lighter outlines. A semi-transparent violet band (#6641F3 at 89% opacity) forms the footer. Bottom-left is the white SecurityScorecard logo: a hexagonal emblem followed by the wordmark 'SecurityScorecard'. No photographs, charts, or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid indigo #34217C with a faint white hexagon honeycomb pattern at 3% opacity. Solid deep indigo background (#34217C) with a subtle white hexagon honeycomb pattern at 3% opacity. The headline is white (#FFFFFF) in TitilliumWeb Regular, a sans-serif, at about 5.9% of slide height (cap height roughly 4.1%). The URL is bold TitilliumWeb in gold/yellow #E1C048 at the same size. The copyright and slide number are white Montserrat, sans-serif, at about 1.2% and 2.3% of slide height. The footer band is violet #6641F3 at 89% opacity. Shapes are flat with no corner radii, shadows, or gradients.
